# Event scripts: `<event.h>`

Natives for event scripts: towns, maps, cutscenes and dungeon systems.

Natives are engine functions, declared extern with their number in NATIVE(...). A native with one result returns it (count = get_counter(3);); natives with several results take pointers (get_actor_pos(0, &x, &y, &z);). Natives push nothing on the VM stack: a native in an expression is called first, into a hidden local, and the expression reads that. OPTIONAL(...) lists arguments a call may leave out. Natives never wait; scripts wait with `wait;`, which yields one frame (25 a second on PAL). While the player skips a cutscene the engine does not run natives until the script reaches `skip_end;`.

NATIVE(B, N) means the game's scripts call the native as B + N. A call can pick another form with an AT(B) or DIRECT prefix.

The declarations are in `sdk/event.h` and `sdk/helpers/event.h`; [the SDK](../sdk.md) explains how they are written, and [the API](README.md) lists the other headers. Generated by `dc2tk api-docs` from the SDK's headers; change the headers, not this page.

- [Natives](#natives): 698
- [Helpers](#helpers): 54
- [Programs](#programs): 203
- [Constants](#constants): 356
- [Value kinds](#value-kinds): 13

| [`move_image_to`](#move_image_to) | 324 | Slides an event image's top-left corner to screen x, y over the given number of frames; 0 jumps there. |
| [`fade_image`](#fade_image) | 325 | Fades an event image's alpha in to 128 (fade_in = 1) or out to 0 (fade_in = 0) over the given number of frames. |
| [`set_image_color`](#set_image_color) | 326 | Sets an event image's colour and alpha, each 0 to 128 where 128 is full. |
| [`init_sprite`](#init_sprite) | 350 | Resets an event sprite (0 to 47) to a hidden, untextured, white screen sprite. |
| [`set_sprite_draw`](#set_sprite_draw) | 351 | Sets the draw pass of an event sprite: 0 hidden, 1 normal pass, 2 first pass. |
| [`set_sprite_type`](#set_sprite_type) | 352 | Sets where an event sprite lives: 0 on screen (screen position, turns around the screen z axis), 1 in the world as a depth-tested billboard. |
| [`set_sprite_texture`](#set_sprite_texture) | 353 | Gives an event sprite the named texture from a texture block. |
| [`set_sprite_pos`](#set_sprite_pos) | 354 | Sets the centre of an event sprite: screen pixels for screen sprites (y is shifted down 32), world units for world sprites. |
| [`set_sprite_size`](#set_sprite_size) | 355 | Sets an event sprite's width and height before scaling. |
| [`set_sprite_uv`](#set_sprite_uv) | 356 | Sets the part of the texture an event sprite shows, in texels: u, v, width, height. |
| [`set_sprite_color`](#set_sprite_color) | 357 | Sets an event sprite's colour and alpha, with 128.0 as full. |
| [`set_sprite_scale`](#set_sprite_scale) | 358 | Sets an event sprite's horizontal and vertical scale. |
| [`set_sprite_blend`](#set_sprite_blend) | 359 | Sets an event sprite's alpha blend mode: 1 normal, 2 additive, 3 subtractive, 4 none, 5 full additive. |
| [`is_camera_path_running`](#is_camera_path_running) | 400 | Returns 1 while the camera sequence is still following its path, 0 once it has reached the end. |
| [`clear_camera_seq`](#clear_camera_seq) | 401 | Stops every track of the event camera sequence, clears its state and drops all queued camera commands. |
| [`delay_camera_moves`](#delay_camera_moves) | 402 | Queues a wait of a number of frames on the camera position track, so the next queued eye or look-at command starts that much later. |
| [`set_camera_seq_pos`](#set_camera_seq_pos) | 403 | Queues putting the camera eye at a world position at once, on the camera position track. |
| [`set_camera_seq_ref`](#set_camera_seq_ref) | 404 | Queues putting the camera look-at point at a world position at once, on the camera position track. |
| [`move_camera_to`](#move_camera_to) | 405 | Queues moving the camera eye to (x, y, z) and the look-at point to (ref_x, ref_y, ref_z) at an even speed over a number of frames. |
| [`move_camera_ref`](#move_camera_ref) | 406 | Queues moving only the camera look-at point to a world position at an even speed over a number of frames. |
| [`clear_camera_path`](#clear_camera_path) | 407 | Queues removing every point from the camera path, on the camera position track. |
| [`set_camera_path_frames`](#set_camera_path_frames) | 408 | Queues setting how many frames the camera takes to run the whole camera path. |
| [`add_camera_path_point`](#add_camera_path_point) | 409 | Queues adding an eye point (x, y, z) and a look-at point (ref_x, ref_y, ref_z) to the end of the camera path, up to 16 points. |
| [`start_camera_path`](#start_camera_path) | 410 | Queues running the camera along a spline through the camera path points over the path's frame count; the position track continues once the end is reached. |
| [`delay_camera_orbit`](#delay_camera_orbit) | 411 | Queues a wait of a number of frames on the camera orbit track (angle, height and distance), so the next queued orbit command starts that much later. |
| [`set_camera_orbit_angle`](#set_camera_orbit_angle) | 412 | Queues placing the camera eye at an angle around the look-at point, keeping its distance. |
| [`set_camera_orbit_height`](#set_camera_orbit_height) | 413 | Queues placing the camera eye a height in world units above the look-at point. |
| [`set_camera_orbit_dist`](#set_camera_orbit_dist) | 414 | Queues placing the camera eye a horizontal distance in world units from the look-at point, keeping its angle. |
| [`set_camera_orbit`](#set_camera_orbit) | 415 | Queues placing the camera eye around the look-at point by angle in radians about the vertical axis, height above it and horizontal distance from it, all at once. |
| [`move_camera_orbit`](#move_camera_orbit) | 416 | Queues changing the camera's angle (radians), height and distance around the look-at point to new values at an even speed over a number of frames. |
| [`delay_camera_fade`](#delay_camera_fade) | 419 | Queues a wait of a number of frames on the camera fade track, so the next queued fade starts that much later. |
| [`init_camera_fade`](#init_camera_fade) | 420 | Queues a fade track command that does nothing and finishes at once. |
| [`fade_camera_in`](#fade_camera_in) | 421 | Queues fading the screen in from a colour over a number of frames, on the camera fade track. |
| [`fade_camera_out`](#fade_camera_out) | 422 | Queues fading the screen out to a colour over a number of frames, on the camera fade track. |
| [`slow_camera_move`](#slow_camera_move) | 423 | Queues letting the camera's last eye and look-at movement carry on and die away: each frame for a number of frames the movement per frame is multiplied by rate (such as 0.96) and applied. |
| [`slow_camera_orbit`](#slow_camera_orbit) | 424 | Queues letting the camera's last angle, height and distance change carry on and die away: each frame for a number of frames the change per frame is multiplied by rate and applied. |
| [`mark_camera_orbit_loop`](#mark_camera_orbit_loop) | 425 | Queues marking a return point on the camera orbit track; commands after it are kept so a later loop_camera_orbit can run them again. |
| [`loop_camera_orbit`](#loop_camera_orbit) | 426 | Queues jumping the camera orbit track back to the return point set by mark_camera_orbit_loop, repeating the commands after it. |
| [`mark_camera_move_loop`](#mark_camera_move_loop) | 427 | Queues marking a return point on the camera position track; commands after it are kept so a later loop_camera_moves can run them again. |
| [`loop_camera_moves`](#loop_camera_moves) | 428 | Queues jumping the camera position track back to the return point set by mark_camera_move_loop, repeating the commands after it. |
| [`delay_camera_shake`](#delay_camera_shake) | 429 | Queues a wait of a number of frames on the camera shake track, so the next queued shake starts that much later. |
| [`shake_camera`](#shake_camera) | 430 | Queues shaking the camera eye and look-at point by an offset (x, y, z in world units) that alternates direction every two frames and shrinks to nothing over a number of frames. |
| [`move_camera_eased`](#move_camera_eased) | 431 | Queues moving the camera eye to (x, y, z) and the look-at point to (ref_x, ref_y, ref_z) over a number of frames with eased speed. |
| [`move_camera_orbit_eased`](#move_camera_orbit_eased) | 432 | Queues changing the camera's angle (radians), height and distance around the look-at point over a number of frames with eased speed. |
| [`delay_camera_chara`](#delay_camera_chara) | 433 | Queues a wait of a number of frames on the camera character track, so the next queued character attach starts that much later. |
| [`attach_chara_to_camera`](#attach_chara_to_camera) | 434 | Queues keeping a character at a distance in world units in front of the camera eye, along the line to the look-at point, for a number of frames. |
| [`move_camera_pos`](#move_camera_pos) | 435 | Queues moving only the camera eye to a world position at an even speed over a number of frames. |
| [`shake_camera_target`](#shake_camera_target) | 436 | Queues shaking only the camera look-at point by an offset (x, y, z in world units) that flips direction every frame and shrinks to nothing over a number of frames. |
| [`check_actor_busy`](#check_actor_busy) | 450 | Returns 1 while any track of the actor's sequence still has queued commands running, and 0 once every track has finished. |
| [`clear_actor_seq`](#clear_actor_seq) | 451 | Stops every track of the actor's sequence, drops its queued commands, forgets which event object it drives and clears its position and rotation. |
| [`bind_actor_object`](#bind_actor_object) | 452 | Makes the actor's sequence drive an event object handle, set up earlier by the event object handle natives. |
| [`delay_actor_moves`](#delay_actor_moves) | 453 | Queues a wait of a number of frames on the actor's position track, so the next queued position command starts that much later. |
| [`set_actor_pos`](#set_actor_pos) | 454 | Queues putting the actor at a world position at once. |
| [`move_actor_to`](#move_actor_to) | 455 | Queues moving the actor to a world position at an even speed over a number of frames. |
| [`clear_actor_path`](#clear_actor_path) | 456 | Queues removing every point from the actor's walking path. |
| [`set_actor_path_frames`](#set_actor_path_frames) | 457 | Queues setting how many frames the actor takes to walk its whole path. |
| [`add_actor_path_point`](#add_actor_path_point) | 458 | Queues adding a world position to the end of the actor's walking path, up to 16 points. |
| [`start_actor_path`](#start_actor_path) | 459 | Queues walking the actor along a spline through its path points over the path's frame count, turning it to face the direction of travel. |
| [`delay_actor_turns`](#delay_actor_turns) | 460 | Queues a wait of a number of frames on the actor's rotation track, so the next queued rotation command starts that much later. |
| [`set_actor_rot`](#set_actor_rot) | 461 | Queues setting the actor's rotation at once, in radians about the x, y and z axes. |
| [`turn_actor_to`](#turn_actor_to) | 462 | Queues turning the actor to a rotation (radians) at an even speed over a number of frames. |
| [`turn_actor_toward`](#turn_actor_toward) | 463 | Queues turning the actor to face a world position over a number of frames. |
| [`delay_actor_motion`](#delay_actor_motion) | 464 | Queues a wait of a number of frames on the actor's motion track, so the next queued motion command starts that much later. |
| [`set_actor_motion`](#set_actor_motion) | 465 | Queues starting a named motion on the actor at once. |
| [`queue_actor_motion`](#queue_actor_motion) | 466 | Queues a named motion that starts once the actor's current motion ends. |
| [`wait_actor_motion_end`](#wait_actor_motion_end) | 467 | Queues holding the actor's motion track until its current motion ends. |
| [`trigger_actor_motion_seq`](#trigger_actor_motion_seq) | 468 | Queues telling the actor's motion sequence to move on to its next part. |
| [`set_actor_blend_speed`](#set_actor_blend_speed) | 469 | Queues setting how fast the actor blends from one motion into the next; values of 0 or below become the default 0.2. |
| [`delay_actor_texture_anim`](#delay_actor_texture_anim) | 470 | Queues a wait of a number of frames on the actor's texture animation track, so the next queued texture animation command starts that much later. |
| [`set_actor_texture_anim`](#set_actor_texture_anim) | 471 | Queues turning a texture animation on the actor on or off. |
| [`wait_actor_motion_trigger`](#wait_actor_motion_trigger) | 472 | Queues holding the actor's motion track until its motion sequence reaches its trigger state (status 3). |
| [`jump_actor_to`](#jump_actor_to) | 473 | Queues moving the actor to a world position along a jump arc over a number of frames. |
| [`delay_actor_color`](#delay_actor_color) | 474 | Queues a wait of a number of frames on the actor's colour track, so the next queued colour change starts that much later. |
| [`set_actor_color`](#set_actor_color) | 475 | Queues changing the colour and alpha of the actor over a number of frames, or at once when frames is 0 or left out. |
| [`reset_actor_motion`](#reset_actor_motion) | 476 | Queues putting the actor's motion back to its start. |
| [`delay_actor_scale`](#delay_actor_scale) | 477 | Queues a wait of a number of frames on the actor's scale track, so the next queued scale change starts that much later. |
| [`set_actor_scale`](#set_actor_scale) | 478 | Queues changing the actor's scale on each axis over a number of frames, or at once when frames is 0 or left out. |
| [`set_actor_motion_time`](#set_actor_motion_time) | 479 | Queues jumping the actor's current motion to a time, in motion frames from its start. |
| [`set_actor_motion_ratio`](#set_actor_motion_ratio) | 481 | Queues jumping the actor's current motion to a share of its length, from 0.0 at the start to 1.0 at the end. |
| [`set_actor_motion_speed`](#set_actor_motion_speed) | 482 | Queues setting how many motion frames the actor's motion advances each step; 1.0 is normal speed. |
| [`attach_actor_to_frame`](#attach_actor_to_frame) | 483 | Queues keeping the actor at a named frame (bone) of another event object handle, plus an offset, for a number of frames: 0 places it once, -1 keeps it there until the sequence is cleared. |
| [`add_actor_pos`](#add_actor_pos) | 484 | Queues adding an offset (world units) to the actor's position every frame for a number of frames, 1 when left out. |
| [`delay_actor_se`](#delay_actor_se) | 485 | Queues a wait of a number of frames on the actor's sound effect track, so the next queued sound effect plays that much later. |
| [`play_actor_se`](#play_actor_se) | 486 | Queues playing a sound effect, by its number within a loaded sound bank, on the actor's sound effect track. |
| [`reset_actor_dynamics`](#reset_actor_dynamics) | 487 | Queues writing the actor's sequence position and rotation to its object and putting its dynamic- animation parts (swinging hair, cloth and the like) back to rest. |
| [`move_actor_eased`](#move_actor_eased) | 488 | Queues moving the actor to a world position over a number of frames with eased speed. |
| [`attach_actor_to_camera`](#attach_actor_to_camera) | 489 | Queues keeping the actor at a distance in world units in front of the active camera eye, along its view line, for a number of frames; -1 keeps it there until the sequence is cleared. |
| [`turn_actor_eased`](#turn_actor_eased) | 490 | Queues turning the actor to a rotation (radians) over a number of frames with eased speed. |
| [`step_actor_motion`](#step_actor_motion) | 491 | Queues stepping the actor's motion once right away, so a newly set motion's pose shows without waiting for the next frame. |
| [`get_contents_pos`](#get_contents_pos) | 501 | Does nothing. |
| [`get_pot_pos`](#get_pot_pos) | 502 | Writes the world position of the pot or box the player carries and throws in dungeons into x, y and z. |
| [`get_pot_state`](#get_pot_state) | 503 | Returns the state of the carried pot: 0 none, 1 held, 2 thrown and flying. |
| [`get_person_status`](#get_person_status) | 504 | Does nothing and reports failure; the result is left unchanged. |
| [`get_player_chara`](#get_player_chara) | 505 | Returns the number of the character the player controls in the event scene. |
| [`get_prev_camera_pos`](#get_prev_camera_pos) | 506 | Writes the eye position of the event scene's previous camera (the one set_prev_camera_id names) into x, y and z. |
| [`get_prev_camera_ref`](#get_prev_camera_ref) | 507 | Writes the look-at position of the event scene's previous camera (the one set_prev_camera_id names) into x, y and z. |
| [`set_camera_next_ref`](#set_camera_next_ref) | 508 | Gives the active follow camera a look-at position it moves toward. |
| [`open_menu`](#open_menu) | 509 | Asks the game to open a menu once the event yields. |
| [`get_menu_status`](#get_menu_status) | 510 | Does nothing and reports failure. |
| [`load_equip_model`](#load_equip_model) | 511 | Loads the model of a party member's current equipment for an event character, using memory stack stack_no. |
| [`get_equip_item`](#get_equip_item) | 512 | Returns the item number in an equipment slot (0 to 4; 0 and 1 are the weapons) of party member 0 or 1. |
| [`set_time_flow`](#set_time_flow) | 513 | Turns the passing of the time of day in the event scene on (1) or off (0). |
| [`set_door_material`](#set_door_material) | 514 | Does nothing. |
| [`enable_scene_skip`](#enable_scene_skip) | 515 | Lets the player skip the current cutscene, fading to black when skipped. |
| [`set_active_camera_id`](#set_active_camera_id) | 516 | Sets which camera number of the event scene is the active one. |
| [`set_prev_camera_id`](#set_prev_camera_id) | 517 | Sets which camera number of the event scene counts as the previous camera, the one the get_prev_camera natives read. |
| [`load_event_dng_map`](#load_event_dng_map) | 519 | Loads a dungeon map for display in an event into memory stack stack_no, from event texture file tex_index, and builds the path the player's piece moves along from room to next_room. |
| [`unload_event_dng_map`](#unload_event_dng_map) | 520 | Frees the textures of the event dungeon map. |
| [`move_dng_map_piece`](#move_dng_map_piece) | 521 | Starts (1) or stops (0) the player's piece moving along its path on the event dungeon map. |
| [`show_event_dng_map`](#show_event_dng_map) | 522 | Shows (non-zero) or hides (0) the event dungeon map. |
| [`fade_event_dng_map`](#fade_event_dng_map) | 523 | Shows the event dungeon map and fades it in (fade_in = 1) or out (fade_in = 0) over a number of frames. |
| [`get_prev_eye_goal`](#get_prev_eye_goal) | 524 | Writes the eye position the event scene's previous camera is moving toward into x, y and z. |
| [`get_prev_ref_goal`](#get_prev_ref_goal) | 525 | Writes the look-at position the event scene's previous camera is moving toward into x, y and z. |
| [`set_camera_goal_pos`](#set_camera_goal_pos) | 526 | Gives the active follow camera an eye position it moves toward. |
| [`check_line_hit`](#check_line_hit) | 527 | Casts a line segment from the from point to the to point against the map's collision polygons, skipping polygons whose ignore bits overlap ignore_mask, and also against treasure boxes when check_boxes is 1. |
| [`set_follow_pos`](#set_follow_pos) | 528 | Sets the world point the active follow camera looks at and trails. |
| [`set_follow_pos_a`](#set_follow_pos_a) | 529 | Sets the world point the active follow camera looks at and trails. |
| [`set_follow_offset`](#set_follow_offset) | 530 | Sets an offset in world units that the active follow camera adds to its follow point. |
| [`set_camera_follow`](#set_camera_follow) | 531 | Turns following on (1) or off (any other value) for the active follow camera. |
| [`step_follow_camera`](#step_follow_camera) | 532 | Advances the active follow camera by a number of frames toward its follow point and target angle. |
| [`set_follow_angle`](#set_follow_angle) | 533 | Sets the angle in radians around the follow point that the active follow camera turns toward. |
| [`set_follow_height`](#set_follow_height) | 534 | Sets how far above the follow point the active follow camera sits, in world units. |
| [`set_follow_distance`](#set_follow_distance) | 535 | Sets how far the active follow camera stays from its follow point, in world units. |
| [`get_direction_angle`](#get_direction_angle) | 536 | Computes the direction from the first point to the second. |
| [`set_dungeon_stage`](#set_dungeon_stage) | 537 | Stores a stage number in the dungeon record of the save data. |
| [`get_dungeon_stage`](#get_dungeon_stage) | 538 | Returns the stage number stored in the dungeon record of the save data. |
| [`set_camera_control`](#set_camera_control) | 539 | Turns the player's camera control on or off for the active camera. |
| [`get_follow_angle`](#get_follow_angle) | 540 | Returns the current angle in radians of the active follow camera around its follow point. |
| [`get_follow_height`](#get_follow_height) | 541 | Returns the height of the active follow camera above its follow point, in world units. |
| [`get_follow_distance`](#get_follow_distance) | 542 | Returns the distance of the active follow camera from its follow point, in world units. |
| [`find_invention_index`](#find_invention_index) | 543 | Looks up an item in the list of inventions the player has made. |
| [`run_pending_map_jump`](#run_pending_map_jump) | 544 | Carries out the map jump stored on the scene when one is pending: it leaves the current interior when the stored target is the exit name, otherwise it enters the named interior map. |
| [`start_door_mode`](#start_door_mode) | 545 | Hands control to the door sequence for the door the scene last touched, passing that door's place numbers, position and facing angle. |
| [`get_money`](#get_money) | 546 | Returns how much money the player has. |
| [`add_money`](#add_money) | 547 | Adds money to the player's purse. |
| [`get_item_count`](#get_item_count) | 548 | Returns how many of an item the player holds. |
| [`check_button_pressed`](#check_button_pressed) | 549 | Checks whether a pad button went down this frame. |
| [`get_language`](#get_language) | 550 | Returns the game language: 0 Japanese, 1 English, 2 French, 3 German, 4 Italian, 5 Spanish. |
| [`check_invention_ideas`](#check_invention_ideas) | 551 | Checks whether the player has the three ideas an invention item needs, from photos or memo entries. |
| [`set_villager_ai`](#set_villager_ai) | 552 | Lets a registered villager move on its own again (on = 1) or holds it in place (on = 0). |
| [`count_photos_of`](#count_photos_of) | 553 | Counts the photos in the player's photo slots that show a subject. |
| [`get_photo_count`](#get_photo_count) | 554 | Returns how many photo slots hold a photo. |
| [`set_contents_misc`](#set_contents_misc) | 555 | Does nothing. |
| [`set_scene_status`](#set_scene_status) | 556 | Sets or clears status bits on a scene entry. |
| [`start_subgame`](#start_subgame) | 557 | Starts a minigame: 1 fishing, 2 fish race, 3 buggy game. |
| [`set_fish_race_value`](#set_fish_race_value) | 558 | Sets fish race state by command: 0 aquarium number, 1 ranking, 2 class, 3 race progress, each from value; 4 adds 1 to the tournament count; 7 reloads the race prizes. |
| [`get_fish_race_value`](#get_fish_race_value) | 559 | Reads fish race state into value by command: 0 aquarium number, 1 ranking, 2 class, 3 race progress, 5 tournament count. |
| [`set_save_value`](#set_save_value) | 560 | Changes save data by command: 0 sets the game progress to value; 1 unlocks the badge of monster value; 2 repairs every ridepod part and refills its energy; 3 sets a flag that makes the next load_bgm do nothing; 4 removes the first electric fish from the aquarium. |
| [`get_save_value`](#get_save_value) | 561 | Reads save data into value by command: 0 game progress, 2 whether the first aquarium tank holds an electric fish, 4 play time in frames, 5 the caption-off setting, 6 the current fishing tournament kind. |
| [`clear_monsters`](#clear_monsters) | 562 | Removes every dungeon monster and clears ridepod projectiles, then resets monster memory, collision, effects, monster textures and the monster sound port. |
| [`set_menu_value`](#set_menu_value) | 563 | Menu helpers by command: 0 (prompt, answer, code) sets the text a name entry checks against; 1 clears fish fatigue and ends the current fishing tournament; 3 shows (1) or hides (0) the Geostone parts announcement; 4 resets the loading bar; 5 (stack, town) builds a town's Geostone announcement in a scene memory stack and starts the download effect; 7 clears the announcement. |
| [`get_menu_value`](#get_menu_value) | 564 | Menu queries by command, written to value: 0 floors with a cleared spheda challenge, 1 the town square event type, 3 scoops newly marked known, 4 scoops newly marked obtained, 8 1 once the Geostone download effect has ended. |
| [`get_analysis_flag`](#get_analysis_flag) | 565 | Checks one town analysis entry. |
| [`get_town_percent`](#get_town_percent) | 566 | Returns a town's Georama completion percentage, the sum of the percent of every analysis entry that is met. |
| [`run_georama_command`](#run_georama_command) | 567 | Georama helpers by command: 1 (slot) loads the current villager's model into that character slot at the town's start point; 2 reloads the NPC living in the placed house into slot 8; 3 (&can_burn) gives whether any placed part can burn. |
| [`find_chara_slot`](#find_chara_slot) | 568 | Searches scene character slots 8 to 63 for the character with this number. |
| [`load_monster_talk_file`](#load_monster_talk_file) | 569 | Starts a background load of the current dungeon's monster talk message file in the current language. |
| [`load_monster_talk_message`](#load_monster_talk_message) | 570 | Copies the loaded monster talk file into a message window's text data, allocating from a scene memory stack. |
| [`start_georama_mode`](#start_georama_mode) | 571 | Leaves the event for Georama edit mode. |
| [`get_chapter`](#get_chapter) | 572 | Returns the story chapter derived from the game progress: 0 below progress 2, 1 at progress 2 or 3, otherwise progress minus 2. |
| [`get_npc_train_value`](#get_npc_train_value) | 573 | Returns a value from a built-in table of NPC train data. |
| [`register_villager`](#register_villager) | 574 | Registers a villager in a scene character slot. |
| [`set_eye_view_parts`](#set_eye_view_parts) | 575 | Switches between two named part groups on the active maps for the first-person view. |
| [`set_quest_state`](#set_quest_state) | 576 | Changes a quest request by command: 0 sets its accepted flag to flag, 1 marks it cleared and ignores flag. |
| [`get_quest_state`](#get_quest_state) | 577 | Returns a quest request's state; command must be 0. |
| [`check_pipe_hit`](#check_pipe_hit) | 578 | Like check_line_hit but sweeps a pipe of the given radius from the from point to the to point, collecting up to 16 hits. |
| [`get_last_interior_map`](#get_last_interior_map) | 579 | Returns the number of the interior map the player last left, or -1 while still inside an interior. |
| [`set_event_data`](#set_event_data) | 581 | Writes one field of the scene's current event data: 0 flag, 1 event number, 2 point number, 3 to 5 event arguments 1 to 3, 6 map event check type, 7 map event number, 13 character slot, 14 character number. |
| [`run_stopwatch`](#run_stopwatch) | 582 | Stopwatch by command: 0 starts timing against the play time; 1 stops it and writes minutes, seconds, hundredths, and 1 when under 7261 frames, or -1 into value1 if it was never started. |
| [`request_edit_event`](#request_edit_event) | 583 | Asks the event loop to act on the town's edit event: 0 sets a scene map flag and restarts it, 1 resets it. |
| [`get_fishing_tournament_value`](#get_fishing_tournament_value) | 584 | Fishing tournament queries by command: 0 (message) writes the top-ten record table into a message window; 2, 3 and 4 write the free entry places, the total weight of the three heaviest fish, and the rank into value. |
| [`set_chara_draw_distance`](#set_chara_draw_distance) | 585 | Sets the camera distance beyond which a character is not drawn, in world units, on the character and all its linked parts. |
| [`set_chara_lighting`](#set_chara_lighting) | 586 | Turns scene lighting on or off for a character's model, or for one named frame of it. |
| [`set_chara_flat_color`](#set_chara_flat_color) | 587 | Sets the flat color of a character's model, or of one named frame of it. |

| [`set_chara_scene_type`](#set_chara_scene_type) | 1034 | Sets the scene type of a character slot. |

| [`set_prev_floor`](#set_prev_floor) | 1036 | Stores the previous floor number of the current dungeon in the save data. |
| [`get_prev_floor`](#get_prev_floor) | 1037 | Returns the previous floor number of the current dungeon from the save data. |
| [`set_fast_floor`](#set_fast_floor) | 1038 | Stub left in the engine: it does nothing and always reports failure. |
| [`set_floor_info`](#set_floor_info) | 1039 | Writes one field of a dungeon floor's save record. |
| [`get_floor_info`](#get_floor_info) | 1040 | Returns one field of a dungeon floor's save record, using the same field numbers as set_floor_info; field 5 gives all the progress flag bits. |
| [`get_next_floor`](#get_next_floor) | 1041 | Returns the floor that follows floor along the given route of the current dungeon map, or 0 when the floor is unknown. |
| [`disable_pad_auto_repeat`](#disable_pad_auto_repeat) | 1042 | Turns off auto-repeat for held controller buttons. |
| [`set_pad_auto_repeat`](#set_pad_auto_repeat) | 1043 | Turns on auto-repeat for the controller buttons in mask, firing after delay frames and then every interval frames while held. |
| [`set_dungeon_pause`](#set_dungeon_pause) | 1044 | Sets (on = 1) or clears (on = 0) dungeon pause bits. |
| [`check_dungeon_pause`](#check_dungeon_pause) | 1045 | Returns the dungeon pause bits that are set within mask; nonzero means at least one is set. |
| [`reset_dungeon_timer`](#reset_dungeon_timer) | 1046 | Resets the dungeon frame timer to 0. |
| [`get_dungeon_timer`](#get_dungeon_timer) | 1047 | Returns the number of frames counted since the dungeon timer was last reset. |
| [`load_chara_skin`](#load_chara_skin) | 1048 | Loads a skin onto a character from a pack file into scene memory stack stack_no. |
| [`check_camera_collision`](#check_camera_collision) | 1049 | Tests the line from one point to another against the map's camera collision. |
| [`get_parts_point_pos`](#get_parts_point_pos) | 1050 | Finds the map parts nearest x, y, z within radius and looks up the named function point on it. |
| [`get_random_circle_pos`](#get_random_circle_pos) | 1051 | Reads the world position of random circle 0 to 2 of the floor into the out variables; circle = -1 means the circle the player last stood on. |
| [`disable_random_circle`](#disable_random_circle) | 1052 | Turns off random circle 0 to 2 of the floor; circle = -1 means the circle the player last stood on. |
| [`swap_map_lighting`](#swap_map_lighting) | 1053 | Swaps the first eight lighting entries of the active map with the next eight, if the map has at least sixteen. |
| [`stop_geostone_anim`](#stop_geostone_anim) | 1054 | Stops the geostone floating up and down. |
| [`set_geostone_flag`](#set_geostone_flag) | 1055 | Sets whether the geostone is on the floor. |
| [`attach_geostone`](#attach_geostone) | 1056 | Attaches the geostone model to a named frame of a character's model so it follows it, such as "item" for a held item. |
| [`detach_geostone`](#detach_geostone) | 1057 | Detaches the geostone model from the character frame set by attach_geostone. |
| [`get_player_move_type`](#get_player_move_type) | 1058 | Returns how the player character moves: 0 on foot, 1 or 4 ridepod on legs, 2 or 5 on treads, 3 on wheels, 6 through the air. |
| [`set_exit_flag`](#set_exit_flag) | 1059 | Sets the scene's exit flag, which event scripts read back with get_exit_flag. |
| [`get_exit_flag`](#get_exit_flag) | 1060 | Returns the scene's exit flag. |
| [`get_e3_version`](#get_e3_version) | 1061 | Returns whether this is the E3 show demo build. |
| [`check_pad_button`](#check_pad_button) | 1062 | Returns the state of logical pad button 0 to 127 this frame; nonzero when the button fires according to its binding. |
| [`stop_control_camera`](#stop_control_camera) | 1063 | Stops the player-controlled camera from moving. |
| [`set_dungeon_brightness`](#set_dungeon_brightness) | 1064 | Sets the scale applied to the colours the dungeon is drawn with; 1.0 is normal. |
| [`get_random_circle_trap`](#get_random_circle_trap) | 1065 | Rolls and returns a random trap number for a random circle of kind 0 or 1. |
| [`apply_random_circle_trap`](#apply_random_circle_trap) | 1066 | Applies the effect of a random circle trap to the active character. |
| [`show_status_bar`](#show_status_bar) | 1067 | Slides the dungeon status bar in (show = 1) or out (show = 0). |
| [`spawn_pull_items`](#spawn_pull_items) | 1068 | Scatters count pickups from x, y, z that fly to the player. |
| [`open_chara_change_menu`](#open_chara_change_menu) | 1069 | Opens the dungeon character change menu; the script is suspended while it is open. |
| [`get_sound_port_bank`](#get_sound_port_bank) | 1070 | Returns the sound bank handle loaded into sound port 0 to 11. |
| [`get_parts_pos`](#get_parts_pos) | 1071 | Finds map parts in the active maps by number or by name and reads its position into the out variables. |
| [`disable_scene_skip`](#disable_scene_skip) | 1072 | Makes the running drama scene unskippable by clearing its skip state. |
| [`get_circle_motion_frame`](#get_circle_motion_frame) | 1073 | Returns the current motion frame of the random circle model. |
| [`set_chara_motion_frame`](#set_chara_motion_frame) | 1074 | Sets the current motion frame of a character, such as to sync it with get_circle_motion_frame. |
| [`update_chara_motion`](#update_chara_motion) | 1075 | Advances a character's motion one step: changes to its pending motion, blends into it and poses the model. |

| [`clear_effect_scripts`](#clear_effect_scripts) | 1102 | Deletes every running effect, unloads every effect script base, empties the work heap, frees their texture blocks and removes the effect script manager. |

| [`delete_effect`](#delete_effect) | 1106 | Deletes the effect in owner's slot at once. |
| [`set_effect_vector1`](#set_effect_vector1) | 1107 | Sets the first work vector of the effect created last, usually its position in world units. |
| [`set_effect_vector2`](#set_effect_vector2) | 1108 | Sets the second work vector of the effect created last, such as a scale or direction, depending on the effect script. |
| [`set_effect_target`](#set_effect_target) | 1109 | Sets the target id of the effect created last; -1 for none. |
| [`load_effect_script_pack`](#load_effect_script_pack) | 1110 | Builds the effect script base effect_name from file_name, which must already be loaded by the background loader or present in the loaded pack. |

| [`set_chara_status`](#set_chara_status) | 1200 | Changes a party character's status condition bits (1 poison, 2 slow, 0x10 power up, others unnamed). |
| [`add_weapon_hp`](#add_weapon_hp) | 1201 | Adds amount to the weapon durability gauge in weapon_slot of the character active in battle; negative values reduce it. |

| [`get_floor_sub_games`](#get_floor_sub_games) | 1209 | Returns the floor's sub games as bits: 1 spheda challenge, 2 fishing record. |

| [`start_screen_wave`](#start_screen_wave) | 1212 | Turns the screen wave on, moving amplitude, speed and pitch to the given values over frames frames (at once when frames is 0 or 1). |
| [`stop_screen_wave`](#stop_screen_wave) | 1213 | Turns the screen wave off, moving amplitude, speed and pitch to the given values over frames frames first (at once when frames is 0 or 1). |

| [`get_debug_flag`](#get_debug_flag) | 1224 | Returns the game's debug flag. |

| [`unlock_stack`](#unlock_stack) | 1233 | Clears the lock on scene memory stack stack_no so it can be reused. |
| [`clear_event_running`](#clear_event_running) | 1234 | Clears the scene's flag that says an event is running. |
| [`set_slot_chara_id`](#set_slot_chara_id) | 1235 | Sets the character id stored in scene character slot. |
| [`get_slot_chara_id`](#get_slot_chara_id) | 1236 | Returns the character id stored in scene character slot, or -1 when the slot does not exist. |

| [`find_near_random_stone`](#find_near_random_stone) | 1238 | Looks for a random stone within 30 units of x, y, z. |
| [`init_mono_flash`](#init_mono_flash) | 1239 | Prepares the mono flash screen effect: two screen sized textures in the first two event texture blocks and their image buffers from scene memory stack stack_no, or the shared read buffer when stack_no is negative. |

| [`end_mono_flash`](#end_mono_flash) | 1241 | Turns the mono flash screen effect off. |
| [`delete_villager`](#delete_villager) | 1242 | Removes the town villager with character id npc from the scene's villager manager. |
| [`set_dungeon_weather`](#set_dungeon_weather) | 1243 | Sets the dungeon weather: 0 normal, 2 rain. |
| [`set_chara_max_hp`](#set_chara_max_hp) | 1244 | Sets a party character's maximum HP and refills HP to it. |
| [`set_chara_defence`](#set_chara_defence) | 1245 | Sets a party character's defence value. |
| [`compare_place_name`](#compare_place_name) | 1246 | Compares the placement name of placed part part_no in the active map with name; returns 0 when they match, nonzero otherwise, and -1 when the part does not exist. |
| [`open_item_choice_menu`](#open_item_choice_menu) | 1247 | Opens the item choice menu and leaves the script so the menu can run; follow with wait. |
| [`set_georama_analyze_flag`](#set_georama_analyze_flag) | 1248 | Sets or clears every georama analysis condition linked to analysis entry entry of map map_no. |
| [`set_atlamillia_visible`](#set_atlamillia_visible) | 1249 | Shows or hides the Atlamillia model parts on scene character actor. |
| [`add_medals`](#add_medals) | 1250 | Adds amount to the player's medal count, kept between 0 and 999. |
| [`set_map_effect_id`](#set_map_effect_id) | 1251 | Sets the dungeon's map effect id. |
| [`get_map_effect_id`](#get_map_effect_id) | 1252 | Returns the dungeon's map effect id. |
| [`init_dungeon_floor`](#init_dungeon_floor) | 1253 | Calls the dungeon floor start hook, which is empty in this build. |
| [`finish_dungeon_floor`](#finish_dungeon_floor) | 1254 | Calls the dungeon floor end hook, which is empty in this build. |
| [`hide_random_stones`](#hide_random_stones) | 1255 | Moves all twelve random stones of the generated floor far below the map, out of play. |
| [`get_floor_status`](#get_floor_status) | 1256 | Returns the current floor's status flags (1 Max blocked, 2 Monica blocked, 4 items blocked, other bits set by scripts). |
| [`set_floor_status`](#set_floor_status) | 1257 | Sets the bits in mask on the current floor's status flags when on is nonzero, clears them when 0. |
| [`get_floor_cell_attr`](#get_floor_cell_attr) | 1258 | Returns the attribute flags of the generated floor's grid cell at a world position, or 1 when the position is off the grid. |
| [`set_actor_near_dist`](#set_actor_near_dist) | 1259 | Turns on fading for scene character actor and sets the distance in world units within which it is not drawn. |
| [`set_saved_time`](#set_saved_time) | 1260 | Stores a time of day in hours in the event info, for a later get_saved_time. |
| [`get_saved_time`](#get_saved_time) | 1261 | Returns the time of day stored with set_saved_time. |
| [`get_door_part`](#get_door_part) | 1262 | Returns the active map's part number of the generated floor's door, or -1 when there is none. |
| [`reset_monica_equipment`](#reset_monica_equipment) | 1263 | Puts Monica's default weapon back on and, unless her costume is flagged to stay, her default costume pieces too. |
| [`add_chara_hp_rate`](#add_chara_hp_rate) | 1264 | Changes a party character's HP by rate times the gauge (for example -0.7 removes 70 percent), never dropping below 1. |

| [`get_chara_weapon_hp`](#get_chara_weapon_hp) | 1267 | Reads the durability of the weapon in weapon_slot (0 or 1) of chara (0 Max, 1 Monica, 2 Ridepod, 3 Monica's monster form). |
| [`add_chara_weapon_hp`](#add_chara_weapon_hp) | 1268 | Adds amount to the durability of the weapon in weapon_slot (0 or 1) of chara (0 Max, 1 Monica, 2 Ridepod, 3 Monica's monster form); negative values reduce it. |
| [`get_chara_weapon_abs`](#get_chara_weapon_abs) | 1269 | Reads the absorption (ABS) points of the weapon in weapon_slot (0 or 1) of chara (0 Max, 1 Monica, 2 Ridepod, 3 Monica's monster form). |
| [`add_chara_weapon_abs`](#add_chara_weapon_abs) | 1270 | Adds amount to the absorption (ABS) points of the weapon in weapon_slot (0 or 1) of chara (0 Max, 1 Monica, 2 Ridepod, 3 Monica's monster form); negative values reduce them. |

| [`set_pause_enabled`](#set_pause_enabled) | 1273 | Allows (1) or blocks (0) the player pausing the game. |
