Sets an offset in world units that the active follow camera adds to its follow point.

Original name: `_SET_FCAMERA_FOLLOW_OFS`.

### set_camera_follow

```c
extern void set_camera_follow(int on) NATIVE(531);
```

Turns following on (1) or off (any other value) for the active follow camera. While off, the camera stays where it is.

Original name: `_SET_FCAMERA_FOLLOW_FLAG`.

### step_follow_camera

```c
extern void step_follow_camera(int frames) NATIVE(532);
```

Advances the active follow camera by a number of frames toward its follow point and target angle. A negative value snaps it there at once.

Original name: `_FCAMERA_STEP`.

### set_follow_angle

```c
extern void set_follow_angle(float angle) NATIVE(533);
```

Sets the angle in radians around the follow point that the active follow camera turns toward. The camera eases to it as it steps.

Original name: `_SET_FCAMERA_ANGLE`.

### set_follow_height

```c
extern void set_follow_height(float height) NATIVE(534);
```

Sets how far above the follow point the active follow camera sits, in world units.

Original name: `_SET_FCAMERA_HEIGHT`.

### set_follow_distance

```c
extern void set_follow_distance(float distance) NATIVE(535);
```

Sets how far the active follow camera stays from its follow point, in world units.

Original name: `_SET_FCAMERA_DIST`.

### get_direction_angle

```c
extern void get_direction_angle(float from_x, float from_y, float from_z, float to_x, float to_y, float to_z, float *yaw, OPTIONAL(float *rot_y, float *rot_z)) NATIVE(536);
```

Computes the direction from the first point to the second. With 7 arguments the last receives the yaw in radians, atan2(dx, dz). With 9 arguments the last three receive a rotation of pitch, yaw and 0.

Original name: `_GET_REF_ANGLE`.

### set_dungeon_stage

```c
extern void set_dungeon_stage(int stage) NATIVE(537);
```

Stores a stage number in the dungeon record of the save data.

Original name: `_DNG_SET_STAGE_ID`.

### get_dungeon_stage

```c
extern int get_dungeon_stage(void) NATIVE(538);
```

Returns the stage number stored in the dungeon record of the save data.

Original name: `_DNG_GET_STAGE_ID`.

### set_camera_control

```c
extern void set_camera_control(int on) NATIVE(539);
```

Turns the player's camera control on or off for the active camera. Nonzero enables both following and pad control; 0 disables both so a script can drive the camera.

Original name: `_SET_CAMERA_CTRL`.

### get_follow_angle

```c
extern float get_follow_angle(void) NATIVE(540);
```

Returns the current angle in radians of the active follow camera around its follow point.

Original name: `_GET_FCAMERA_ANGLE`.

### get_follow_height

```c
extern float get_follow_height(void) NATIVE(541);
```

Returns the height of the active follow camera above its follow point, in world units.

Original name: `_GET_FCAMERA_HEIGHT`.

### get_follow_distance

```c
extern float get_follow_distance(void) NATIVE(542);
```

Returns the distance of the active follow camera from its follow point, in world units.

Original name: `_GET_FCAMERA_DIST`.

### find_invention_index

```c
extern int find_invention_index(item_id item) NATIVE(543);
```

Looks up an item in the list of inventions the player has made. Returns its position in that list, or -1 when it has not been invented.

Original name: `_GET_INVENTION_ID`.

### run_pending_map_jump

```c
extern void run_pending_map_jump(void) NATIVE(544);
```

Carries out the map jump stored on the scene when one is pending: it leaves the current interior when the stored target is the exit name, otherwise it enters the named interior map. Fails when no jump is pending.

Original name: `_FUNCTION_MAP_JUMP`.

### start_door_mode

```c
extern void start_door_mode(void) NATIVE(545);
```

Hands control to the door sequence for the door the scene last touched, passing that door's place numbers, position and facing angle.

Original name: `_FUNCTION_DOOR_MODE`.

### get_money

```c
extern int get_money(void) NATIVE(546);
```

Returns how much money the player has.

Original name: `_GET_MONEY`.

### add_money

```c
extern void add_money(int amount) NATIVE(547);
```

Adds money to the player's purse. A negative amount takes money away.

Original name: `_ADD_MONEY`.

### get_item_count

```c
extern int get_item_count(item_id item) NATIVE(548);
```

Returns how many of an item the player holds.

Original name: `_GET_ITEM_NUM`.

### check_button_pressed

```c
extern int check_button_pressed(enum check_button button) NATIVE(549);
```

Checks whether a pad button went down this frame. button 0 is cancel and 1 is confirm (cross and circle, swapped for Japanese), 2 is square and 3 is select. Returns 1 or 0.

Original name: `_CHECK_BUTTON`.

### get_language

```c
extern int get_language(void) NATIVE(550);
```

Returns the game language: 0 Japanese, 1 English, 2 French, 3 German, 4 Italian, 5 Spanish.

Original name: `_GET_LANGUAGE`.

### check_invention_ideas

```c
extern int check_invention_ideas(item_id item) NATIVE(551);
```

Checks whether the player has the three ideas an invention item needs, from photos or memo entries. Returns 1 when all three are present, else 0.

Original name: `_CHECK_INVENT_ITEM`.

### set_villager_ai

```c
extern void set_villager_ai(int on, int chara) NATIVE(552);
```

Lets a registered villager move on its own again (on = 1) or holds it in place (on = 0). chara is the scene character slot the villager was registered in.

Original name: `_SET_AI`.

### count_photos_of

```c
extern void count_photos_of(int subject, enum photo_subject_kind kind, OPTIONAL(int *count)) NATIVE(553);
```

Counts the photos in the player's photo slots that show a subject. Called as (subject, &count) the subject is an idea number; with three arguments kind picks what subject means: 0 idea number, 2 NPC number, 3 monster number.

Original name: `_CHECK_INVENT_PHOTO`.

### get_photo_count

```c
extern int get_photo_count(void) NATIVE(554);
```

Returns how many photo slots hold a photo.

Original name: `_GET_PHOTO_NUM`.

### set_contents_misc

```c
extern void set_contents_misc(void) NATIVE(555);
```

Does nothing.

Original name: `_SET_CONTENTS_ETC`.

### set_scene_status

```c
extern void set_scene_status(enum scene_data_kind kind, int index, OPTIONAL(enum scene_status bits, int on)) NATIVE(556);
```

Sets or clears status bits on a scene entry. kind picks the list: 1 character, 2 map, 3 message, 4 camera, 6 or 7 game object; on defaults to 1, and 0 clears the bits instead. bits is always read, so a two-argument call reads a stray stack value.

Original name: `_SET_STATUS`.

### start_subgame

```c
extern void start_subgame(enum subgame_type type) NATIVE(557);
```

Starts a minigame: 1 fishing, 2 fish race, 3 buggy game.

Original name: `_GOTO_SUBGAME`.

### set_fish_race_value

```c
extern void set_fish_race_value(enum gyorace_set_command command, OPTIONAL(any value, int name_slot, int entry)) NATIVE(558);
```

Sets fish race state by command: 0 aquarium number, 1 ranking, 2 class, 3 race progress, each from value; 4 adds 1 to the tournament count; 7 reloads the race prizes. Commands 5 and 6 are called as (command, message, name_slot, entry) and copy that race entry's fish name (5) or race time as mm:ss:hh text (6) into the message's name slot, counted from 1.

Original name: `_SET_GYORACE_ETC`.

### get_fish_race_value

```c
extern void get_fish_race_value(enum gyorace_get_command command, any value, OPTIONAL(int place, int *prize_item, int *prize_count)) NATIVE(559);
```

Reads fish race state into value by command: 0 aquarium number, 1 ranking, 2 class, 3 race progress, 5 tournament count. Command 4 is called as (4, race, place, &prize_item, &prize_count) and reads the prize for a place counted from 1.

Original name: `_GET_GYORACE_ETC`.

### set_save_value

```c
extern void set_save_value(enum save_set_command command, OPTIONAL(int value, int unused)) NATIVE(560);
```

Changes save data by command: 0 sets the game progress to value; 1 unlocks the badge of monster value; 2 repairs every ridepod part and refills its energy; 3 sets a flag that makes the next load_bgm do nothing; 4 removes the first electric fish from the aquarium. A third argument is ignored.

Original name: `_SET_SAVEDATA_ETC`.

### get_save_value

```c
extern void get_save_value(enum save_get_command command, any value, OPTIONAL(int *result)) NATIVE(561);
```

Reads save data into value by command: 0 game progress, 2 whether the first aquarium tank holds an electric fish, 4 play time in frames, 5 the caption-off setting, 6 the current fishing tournament kind. Commands 1 and 3 are called as (command, input, &result): 1 gives whether the badge of monster input is unlocked, 3 the bait item on fishing rod input.

Original name: `_GET_SAVEDATA_ETC`.

### clear_monsters

```c
extern void clear_monsters(any unused) NATIVE(562);
```

Removes every dungeon monster and clears ridepod projectiles, then resets monster memory, collision, effects, monster textures and the monster sound port. The argument is not read; the disc passes -1.

Original name: `_DEL_MONSTER`.

### set_menu_value

```c
extern void set_menu_value(enum menu_set_command command, OPTIONAL(any arg1, any arg2, any arg3)) NATIVE(563);
```

Menu helpers by command: 0 (prompt, answer, code) sets the text a name entry checks against; 1 clears fish fatigue and ends the current fishing tournament; 3 shows (1) or hides (0) the Geostone parts announcement; 4 resets the loading bar; 5 (stack, town) builds a town's Geostone announcement in a scene memory stack and starts the download effect; 7 clears the announcement. Commands 2 and 6 do nothing.

Original name: `_SET_MENU_ETC`.

### get_menu_value

```c
extern void get_menu_value(enum menu_get_command command, any value, OPTIONAL(void *value2)) NATIVE(564);
```

Menu queries by command, written to value: 0 floors with a cleared spheda challenge, 1 the town square event type, 3 scoops newly marked known, 4 scoops newly marked obtained, 8 1 once the Geostone download effect has ended. 2 (fish, &size) gives a fish's record size; 5 (&obtained, &total) the scoop totals; 6 (&count, &status) Donny's shop lineup count and status; 7 (confirm, &done) steps the Geostone announcement and gives 1 when it has ended.

Original name: `_GET_MENU_ETC`.

### get_analysis_flag

```c
extern int get_analysis_flag(int analysis_id) NATIVE(565);
```

Checks one town analysis entry. analysis_id is town \* 100 + entry, with towns counted from 1, and the result is whether all of the entry's conditions hold.

Original name: `_GET_ANALYZE`.

### get_town_percent

```c
extern int get_town_percent(int town) NATIVE(566);
```

Returns a town's Georama completion percentage, the sum of the percent of every analysis entry that is met. town counts from 0.

Original name: `_GET_DIORAMA_PERCENT`.

### run_georama_command

```c
extern void run_georama_command(int command, OPTIONAL(any value)) NATIVE(567);
```

Georama helpers by command: 1 (slot) loads the current villager's model into that character slot at the town's start point; 2 reloads the NPC living in the placed house into slot 8; 3 (&can_burn) gives whether any placed part can burn.

Original name: `_GEORAMA_FUNC`.

### find_chara_slot

```c
extern int find_chara_slot(int chara_no) NATIVE(568);
```

Searches scene character slots 8 to 63 for the character with this number. Returns the slot, or -1 when none matches.

Original name: `_GET_CHARA_ID`.

### load_monster_talk_file

```c
extern void load_monster_talk_file(void) NATIVE(569);
```

Starts a background load of the current dungeon's monster talk message file in the current language.

Original name: `_SET_LOADBG_FILE_MONS_TALK`.

### load_monster_talk_message

```c
extern void load_monster_talk_message(int message, enum memory_stack stack) NATIVE(570);
```

Copies the loaded monster talk file into a message window's text data, allocating from a scene memory stack. Use after load_monster_talk_file has finished loading.

Original name: `_LOAD_MES_MONS_TALK`.

### start_georama_mode

```c
extern void start_georama_mode(any unused) NATIVE(571);
```

Leaves the event for Georama edit mode. The argument is not read.

Original name: `_GOTO_EDITMODE`.

### get_chapter

```c
extern int get_chapter(void) NATIVE(572);
```

Returns the story chapter derived from the game progress: 0 below progress 2, 1 at progress 2 or 3, otherwise progress minus 2.

Original name: `_GET_CHAPTER`.

### get_npc_train_value

```c
extern int get_npc_train_value(int column, int npc) NATIVE(573);
```

Returns a value from a built-in table of NPC train data. npc picks the row, counted from 1, and column the value; scripts use column 1 for a seat and 2 for an x offset.

Original name: `_GET_NPC_TRAIN_ETC`.

### register_villager

```c
extern void register_villager(chara_slot slot, npc_id villager, OPTIONAL(int place)) NATIVE(574);
```

Registers a villager in a scene character slot. With a place number it starts at that villager place's position and facing; without, a blank place is allocated from scene memory stack 4.

Original name: `_REGISTER_VILLAGER`.

### set_eye_view_parts

```c
extern void set_eye_view_parts(int on) NATIVE(575);
```

Switches between two named part groups on the active maps for the first-person view. Nonzero shows the first group and hides the second; 0 does the reverse.

Original name: `_EYE_VIEW_DRAW_ON_OFF`.

### set_quest_state

```c
extern void set_quest_state(enum quest_set_command command, int quest, int flag) NATIVE(576);
```

Changes a quest request by command: 0 sets its accepted flag to flag, 1 marks it cleared and ignores flag.

Original name: `_SET_QUEST_ETC`.

### get_quest_state

```c
extern int get_quest_state(enum quest_get_command command, int quest) NATIVE(577);
```

Returns a quest request's state; command must be 0. The result is -1 invalid, 0 not taken, 1 accepted, 2 cleared.

Original name: `_GET_QUEST_ETC`.

### check_pipe_hit

```c
extern void check_pipe_hit(int ignore_mask, float from_x, float from_y, float from_z, float to_x, float to_y, float to_z, float radius, int check_boxes, void *result1, OPTIONAL(void *result2, void *result3, void *result4, void *result5, void *result6, void *result7, void *result8)) NATIVE(578);
```

Like check_line_hit but sweeps a pipe of the given radius from the from point to the to point, collecting up to 16 hits. Here hit is the number of hits and the position, reflection and kind come from the first. The outputs depend on the argument count: 10 writes hit; 11 writes hit, kind; 13 writes hit x, y, z, hit; 14 adds kind; 16 writes hit x, y, z, reflection x, y, z, hit; 17 adds kind.

Original name: `_CHK_INTERSECTION_POINT_PIPE`.

### get_last_interior_map

```c
extern int get_last_interior_map(void) NATIVE(579);
```

Returns the number of the interior map the player last left, or -1 while still inside an interior.

Original name: `_GET_OLD_INTERIOR_MAP_NO`.

### set_event_data

```c
extern void set_event_data(enum event_data_field field, int value) NATIVE(581);
```

Writes one field of the scene's current event data: 0 flag, 1 event number, 2 point number, 3 to 5 event arguments 1 to 3, 6 map event check type, 7 map event number, 13 character slot, 14 character number.

Original name: `_SET_EVENT_DATA`.

### run_stopwatch

```c
extern void run_stopwatch(enum stopwatch_command command, OPTIONAL(void *value1, void *value2, void *value3, int *value4)) NATIVE(582);
```

Stopwatch by command: 0 starts timing against the play time; 1 stops it and writes minutes, seconds, hundredths, and 1 when under 7261 frames, or -1 into value1 if it was never started. 2 (frames) sets the time limit; 3 (x, y, style) sets the screen position and display style.

Original name: `_STOPWATCH`.

### request_edit_event

```c
extern void request_edit_event(enum edit_event_command command) NATIVE(583);
```

Asks the event loop to act on the town's edit event: 0 sets a scene map flag and restarts it, 1 resets it.

Original name: `_SET_FUNC_ETC`.

### get_fishing_tournament_value

```c
extern void get_fishing_tournament_value(enum fish_tour_get_command command, any value, OPTIONAL(int *prize_item, int *prize_count)) NATIVE(584);
```

Fishing tournament queries by command: 0 (message) writes the top-ten record table into a message window; 2, 3 and 4 write the free entry places, the total weight of the three heaviest fish, and the rank into value. 1 is called as (1, place, &prize_item, &prize_count) and reads the prize for a place counted from 1.

Original name: `_GET_FISHINGTOURNAMENT_ETC`.

### set_chara_draw_distance

```c
extern void set_chara_draw_distance(chara_slot chara, float distance) NATIVE(585);
```

Sets the camera distance beyond which a character is not drawn, in world units, on the character and all its linked parts.

Original name: `_SET_CHARA_FAR_DIST`.

### set_chara_lighting

```c
extern void set_chara_lighting(chara_slot chara, int on, OPTIONAL(string frame_name)) NATIVE(586);
```

Turns scene lighting on or off for a character's model, or for one named frame of it. Off draws it flat with neutral color 128.

Original name: `_SET_MODEL_LIGHT_SWITCH`.

### set_chara_flat_color

```c
extern void set_chara_flat_color(chara_slot chara, float r, float g, float b, float a, OPTIONAL(string frame_name)) NATIVE(587);
```

Sets the flat color of a character's model, or of one named frame of it. Each channel runs 0 to 255 with 128 as neutral.

Original name: `_SET_MODEL_LIGHT_COLOR`.

### get_extra_mode

```c
extern int get_extra_mode(void) NATIVE(588);
```

Returns the extra mode flag, nonzero while a bonus mode started from the title screen is being played.

Original name: `_GET_OMAKE_FLAG`.

### set_wind

```c
extern void set_wind(float power, OPTIONAL(float dir_x, float dir_y, float dir_z)) NATIVE(589);
```

Sets the scene wind used by swinging model parts, as a strength and a direction vector that is normalized. A negative power removes the wind, and the direction can then be left out.

Original name: `_SET_WIND`.

### set_extra_mode

```c
extern void set_extra_mode(int flag) NATIVE(590);
```

Sets the extra mode flag that marks a bonus mode started from the title screen.

Original name: `_SET_OMAKE_FLAG`.

### reset_sound_port

```c
extern void reset_sound_port(enum snd_port port) NATIVE(600);
```

Stops every sound on a sound port and reinitializes it. Ports: 0 music, 1 objects, 2 ambient, 3 base map, 4 event, 5 monsters, 6 system, 8 menu, 9 battle, 11 second music.

Original name: `_SND_INIT_PORT`.

### load_sound

```c
extern void load_sound(enum snd_port port, any file, OPTIONAL(int *sound_id)) NATIVE(601);
```

Loads a sound bank onto a port and gives its sound id. Called as (port, file, &sound_id) or as (file, &sound_id) for port 4; file is a path string, or an int sound data number whose event sound file is used. Port 8 uses the second event sound buffer.

Original name: `_SND_LOAD_SOUND`.

### play_se

```c
extern void play_se(int sound_id, int se_no, OPTIONAL(int volume, int pan)) NATIVE(602);
```

Plays sound effect se_no from a loaded sound bank. volume runs 0 to 127 and pan 0 to 127 with 64 centered; without them the bank defaults are used.

Original name: `_SND_SE_PLAY`.

### get_sound_id

```c
extern void get_sound_id(enum se_bank bank, int *sound_id, OPTIONAL(int *source_sound_id)) NATIVE(603);
```

Reads the sound id of a scene sound bank: 0 ambient, 1 to 3 base map, 4 battle. Bank 5 is called as (5, source_no, &source_sound_id) for a sound source; with one argument the id of the last loaded sound is written.

Original name: `_GET_SND_ID`.

### pause_se

```c
extern void pause_se(int sound_id, int se_no) NATIVE(604);
```

Pauses sound effect se_no of a loaded sound bank.

Original name: `_SND_SE_PAUSE`.

### stop_se

```c
extern void stop_se(int sound_id, int se_no) NATIVE(605);
```

Stops sound effect se_no of a loaded sound bank.

Original name: `_SND_SE_STOP`.

### set_se_volume

```c
extern void set_se_volume(int sound_id, int se_no, any volume) NATIVE(606);
```

Sets the volume of a playing sound effect. An int is an absolute volume from 0 to 127; a float scales the effect's default volume, capped at 127.

Original name: `_SND_SET_SE_VOL`.

### set_se_pan

```c
extern void set_se_pan(int sound_id, int se_no, int pan) NATIVE(607);
```

Sets the pan of sound effect se_no in a sound bank, 0 to 127 with 64 centered.

Original name: `_SND_SET_SE_PAN`.

### set_se_pitch

```c
extern void set_se_pitch(int sound_id, int se_no, int pitch) NATIVE(608);
```

Sets the pitch of sound effect se_no in a sound bank.

Original name: `_SND_SET_SE_PITCH`.

### stop_all_se_on_port

```c
extern void stop_all_se_on_port(enum snd_port port) NATIVE(609);
```

Stops every sound effect on a sound port; -1 stops all ports except music.

Original name: `_SND_SE_ALL_STOP`.

### load_bgm

```c
extern void load_bgm(bgm_file bgm_file) NATIVE(610);
```

Loads a music file by number into the scene's music slot. Does nothing when that file is already loaded, or once after set_save_value command 3 set the skip flag.

Original name: `_LOAD_BGM`.

### play_bgm

```c
extern void play_bgm(int track, OPTIONAL(int volume)) NATIVE(611);
```

Plays a track of the loaded music file, stopping the previous one; scripts use track 0. volume runs 0 to 127, and -1 or leaving it out uses the default.

Original name: `_PLAY_BGM`.

### stop_bgm

```c
extern void stop_bgm(int track) NATIVE(612);
```

Stops a track of the loaded music file.

Original name: `_STOP_BGM`.

### open_stream

```c
extern void open_stream(enum stream_channel channel, any voice, OPTIONAL(string base_name)) NATIVE(613);
```

Starts opening the event voice stream. An int voice is a voice number found in the voice packs and a string is a stream file name; with three arguments voice is a pack name and base_name a file inside it. The channel is not used; poll check_stream_busy until it finishes.

Original name: `_STREAM_OPEN`.

### play_stream

```c
extern void play_stream(enum stream_channel channel, OPTIONAL(int volume)) NATIVE(614);
```

Plays the opened event voice stream. Without a volume the maximum stream volume is used; the volume is lowered by the current reverb depth. The channel is not used.

Original name: `_STREAM_PLAY`.

### stop_stream

```c
extern void stop_stream(enum stream_channel channel) NATIVE(615);
```

Stops and closes the event voice stream. The channel is not used.

Original name: `_STREAM_STOP`.

### prepare_stream

```c
extern void prepare_stream(enum stream_channel channel) NATIVE(616);
```

Sets up the opened event voice stream for mono or stereo and starts preloading it. The channel is not used.

Original name: `_STREAM_STANDBY`.

### attach_camera_to_actor

```c
extern void attach_camera_to_actor(int actor, float ofs_x, float ofs_y, float ofs_z, float angle, float height, float distance, OPTIONAL(string frame_name, enum scene_cmr_sync_mode mode)) NATIVE(617);
```

Queues a camera sequence command that keeps the camera orbiting an actor at the actor's position plus the offset, at an angle in radians, a height and a distance in world units. frame_name follows that frame of the model instead (empty for the actor itself); mode 0 keeps the angle fixed, 1 turns with the actor's heading, 2 follows the frame's full orientation.

Original name: `_CMRS_SYNC_OBJ`.

### detach_camera_from_actor

```c
extern void detach_camera_from_actor(void) NATIVE(618);
```

Queues a camera sequence command that stops the camera orbiting an actor.

Original name: `_CMRS_RELEASE_OBJ`.

### get_stream_status

```c
extern int get_stream_status(enum stream_channel channel) NATIVE(619);
```

Returns the state of the event voice stream, 0 when it is idle or finished. The channel is not used.

Original name: `_STREAM_GET_STATUS`.

### get_system_sound_id

```c
extern int get_system_sound_id(void) NATIVE(620);
```

Returns the sound id of the system sound bank, for use with play_se.

Original name: `_GET_SYS_SND_ID`.

### check_stream_busy

```c
extern int check_stream_busy(void) NATIVE(621);
```

Checks whether a stream request is still in progress. Returns nonzero until open_stream or prepare_stream has finished.

Original name: `_STREAM_OPEN_CHECK`.

### load_env_sound

```c
extern void load_env_sound(int bank) NATIVE(622);
```

Loads an ambient sound bank by number, unless it is already loaded.

Original name: `_LOAD_SE_ENV`.

### play_env_sound

```c
extern void play_env_sound(int se_no, OPTIONAL(float volume)) NATIVE(623);
```

Plays an ambient loop from the ambient sound bank, stopping the previous one. -1 switches back to the automatic ambient sound at full volume. volume is truncated to a whole number, so only 0 and 1 are useful.

Original name: `_PLAY_ENV_BGM`.

### play_system_se

```c
extern void play_system_se(system_se se_no) NATIVE(624);
```

Plays a sound effect from the system sound bank. Negative numbers do nothing.

Original name: `_SYS_SE_PLAY`.

### reset_source_sounds

```c
extern void reset_source_sounds(void) NATIVE(625);
```

Stops and unloads all positional sound sources and resets the event and object sound ports.

Original name: `_INIT_SE_SRC`.

### reset_env_sounds

```c
extern void reset_env_sounds(void) NATIVE(626);
```

Stops the ambient loop, unloads the ambient sound bank and resets the sound sources.

Original name: `_INIT_SE_ENV`.

### reset_base_sounds

```c
extern void reset_base_sounds(void) NATIVE(627);
```

Stops and unloads the base map sound bank and the battle sound bank.

Original name: `_INIT_SE_BAS`.

### load_source_sound

```c
extern void load_source_sound(int bank) NATIVE(628);
```

Loads a positional sound source bank by number, unless it is already loaded.

Original name: `_LOAD_SE_SRC`.

### load_footstep_sound

```c
extern void load_footstep_sound(void) NATIVE(629);
```

Does nothing and reports failure.

Original name: `_LOAD_SE_FOOT`.

### load_door_sound

```c
extern void load_door_sound(void) NATIVE(630);
```

Does nothing and reports failure.

Original name: `_LOAD_SE_DOOR`.

### load_box_sound

```c
extern void load_box_sound(void) NATIVE(631);
```

Does nothing and reports failure.

Original name: `_LOAD_SE_BOX`.

### load_battle_sound

```c
extern void load_battle_sound(int bank) NATIVE(632);
```

Loads a battle sound bank by number, unless it is already loaded.

Original name: `_LOAD_SE_BATTLE`.

### delete_sound_port

```c
extern void delete_sound_port(enum snd_port port) NATIVE(633);
```

Releases a sound port in the sound driver, freeing what was loaded on it, and reinitializes the port.

Original name: `_SND_DELETE_PORT`.

### stop_env_sound

```c
extern void stop_env_sound(void) NATIVE(634);
```

Stops the ambient loop.

Original name: `_STOP_ENV_BGM`.

### fade_in_bgm

```c
extern void fade_in_bgm(int frames) NATIVE(635);
```

Fades the music in from silence over a number of frames.

Original name: `_FADE_IN_BGM`.

### fade_out_bgm

```c
extern void fade_out_bgm(int frames) NATIVE(636);
```

Fades the music out over a number of frames.

Original name: `_FADE_OUT_BGM`.

### set_bgm_volume

```c
extern void set_bgm_volume(any volume) NATIVE(637);
```

Sets the volume of the playing music. An int sets it directly (0 to 127, negative restores the bank's default volume); a float multiplies the current volume by that factor.

Original name: `_SET_BGM_VOL`.

### set_sound_reverb

```c
extern void set_sound_reverb(void) NATIVE(638);
```

Does nothing and reports failure. The engine leaves this native empty.

Original name: `_SND_SET_REVERB`.

### set_env_bgm_volume

```c
extern void set_env_bgm_volume(float volume) NATIVE(639);
```

Sets the volume of the map's ambient environment sound loop, as a factor from 0.0 to 1.0.

Original name: `_SND_SET_ENV_VOL`.

### check_stream_audible

```c
extern int check_stream_audible(enum stream_channel channel) NATIVE(640);
```

Reads the output level of the event stream and returns 1 when either channel is outside -10 to 10, 0 when both are near silence. The channel argument is ignored; the event stream is always used.

Original name: `_STREAM_SILENT_CHECK`.

### set_env_bgm_auto

```c
extern void set_env_bgm_auto(int on) NATIVE(641);
```

Turns on (1) or off (0) the environment sound following the time band of the map.

Original name: `_AUTO_CHANGE_ENV`.

### skip_next_bgm_load

```c
extern void skip_next_bgm_load(void) NATIVE(642);
```

Makes the next scene music load be skipped once, keeping the music already loaded. The flag clears itself when used.

Original name: `_BGM_LOAD_CANCEL`.

### skip_next_sound_load

```c
extern void skip_next_sound_load(void) NATIVE(643);
```

Makes the next sound set load (load_sound_set or a map change) be skipped once. The flag clears itself when used.

Original name: `_SOUND_LOAD_CANCEL`.

### allow_bgm_load

```c
extern void allow_bgm_load(void) NATIVE(644);
```

Clears the flag set by skip_next_bgm_load so the next music load happens normally.

Original name: `_BGM_LOAD_ENABLE`.

### allow_sound_load

```c
extern void allow_sound_load(void) NATIVE(645);
```

Clears the flag set by skip_next_sound_load so the next sound set load happens normally.

Original name: `_SOUND_LOAD_ENABLE`.

### load_se_base

```c
extern void load_se_base(int bank_no) NATIVE(646);
```

Loads the base sound effect bank with the given number, unless that bank is already loaded or the number is negative. Reports failure in those cases.

Original name: `_LOAD_SE_BASE`.

### load_sound_set

```c
extern void load_sound_set(sound_set sound_data_id) NATIVE(647);
```

Loads everything listed for a sound data id: the base, battle, environment and source effect banks, the reverb setting and the environment sound loop. Banks already loaded are kept. Skipped once after skip_next_sound_load.

Original name: `_LOAD_SOUND`.

### close_stream

```c
extern void close_stream(enum stream_channel channel) NATIVE(648);
```

Stops and closes the event stream, restoring its saved volume. The channel argument is ignored; the event stream is always used.

Original name: `_STREAM_CLOSE`.

### open_stream_named

```c
extern void open_stream_named(enum stream_channel channel, string file_name) NATIVE(649);
```

Marks the event stream as reading from a file name, but the engine only builds the path and never opens the file. The channel argument is ignored.

Original name: `_STREAM_OPEN2`.

### load_bgm_pack

```c
extern void load_bgm_pack(bgm_file bgm_no) NATIVE(650);
```

Makes the given music number the loaded music pack, taking the file from the preloaded background buffers. Does nothing when that number is already loaded or negative, and fails when the file was not preloaded.

Original name: `_LOAD_BGM_PACK`.

### get_bgm_no

```c
extern int get_bgm_no(...) NATIVE(651);
```

Returns the number of the currently loaded music pack.

Original name: `_GET_BGM_NO`.

### get_bgm_master_volume

```c
extern float get_bgm_master_volume(void) NATIVE(652);
```

Returns the master volume factor of the active music channel.

Original name: `_GET_MASTER_VOL`.

### set_bgm_master_volume

```c
extern void set_bgm_master_volume(float volume) NATIVE(653);
```

Sets the master volume factor of the active music channel and reapplies the music volume. 1.0 is normal.

Original name: `_SET_MASTER_VOL`.

### get_battle_bgm_volume

```c
extern float get_battle_bgm_volume(void) NATIVE(654);
```

Returns the volume the dungeon battle music is played at.

Original name: `_GET_BTL_BGM_VOL`.

### set_battle_bgm_volume

```c
extern void set_battle_bgm_volume(float volume) NATIVE(655);
```

Sets the volume the dungeon battle music is played at.

Original name: `_SET_BTL_BGM_VOL`.

### set_reverb_input

```c
extern void set_reverb_input(int on) NATIVE(656);
```

Sets a sound processor parameter on both cores that the menus also use when switching reverb: nonzero on sets it to -4, 0 sets it to -52.

Original name: `_SND_IN_REVERB`.

### stop_source_se

```c
extern void stop_source_se(void) NATIVE(657);
```

Stops every sound effect still playing from the scene's loaded source effect banks.

Original name: `_SND_STOP_SRC`.

### pause_bgm

```c
extern void pause_bgm(void) NATIVE(658);
```

Pauses the music playing on the active music channel.

Original name: `_SND_PAUSE_BGM`.

### open_stream_wav

```c
extern void open_stream_wav(enum stream_channel channel, int stream_no) NATIVE(659);
```

Opens the numbered stream file (number printed as seven digits plus .wav, such as 5400015.wav) on the event stream for playback. The channel argument is ignored; the event stream is always used.

Original name: `_STREAM_OPEN3`.

### save_bgm_status

```c
extern void save_bgm_status(int unused) NATIVE(660);
```

Saves the active music's state (playing, paused or stopped, track, volumes, loaded pack) into a single save slot that restore_bgm_status reads. The argument is ignored.

Original name: `_GET_ACTIVE_BGM_STATUS`.

### restore_bgm_status

```c
extern void restore_bgm_status(int unused) NATIVE(661);
```

Restores the music state saved by save_bgm_status: track, volumes, and whether it plays, is paused or is stopped. The argument is ignored.

Original name: `_SET_ACTIVE_BGM_STATUS`.

### get_saved_bgm_no

```c
extern int get_saved_bgm_no(void) NATIVE(662);
```

Returns the music pack number stored by the last save_bgm_status.

Original name: `_GET_BGM_STATUS_NOW_NO`.

### get_se_status

```c
extern int get_se_status(int sound_id, int se_no) NATIVE(663);
```

Returns the playback state of a sequence sound: 0 stopped, 1 playing, 2 paused, 3 port paused, or -1 when the sound id or number is invalid or the sound is not a sequence.

Original name: `_GET_SE_STATUS`.

### stop_all_se

```c
extern void stop_all_se(void) NATIVE(664);
```

Stops every sound effect, including source effects and looping effects, and resets the looping effect manager.

Original name: `_SE_ALL_STOP`.

### stop_all_sound

```c
extern void stop_all_sound(void) NATIVE(665);
```

Stops the music, resets the music channel and stops every sound effect.

Original name: `_SOUND_ALL_STOP`.

### skip_next_bgm_play

```c
extern void skip_next_bgm_play(void) NATIVE(666);
```

Makes the next music play request be ignored once. The flag clears itself when used.

Original name: `_BGM_PLAY_CANCEL`.

### allow_bgm_play

```c
extern void allow_bgm_play(void) NATIVE(667);
```

Clears the flag set by skip_next_bgm_play so the next music play request happens normally.

Original name: `_BGM_PLAY_ENABLE`.

### get_map_default_bgm

```c
extern int get_map_default_bgm(map_id map) NATIVE(668);
```

Returns the default music number of a map's sound data, or -1 when it has none. Called without map, it uses the current map (or sub map when one is active).

Original name: `_GET_DEF_BGM_NO`.

### set_movie_caption

```c
extern void set_movie_caption(enum movie_caption_mode mode, int value, OPTIONAL(int start_frame, string text, int frames)) NATIVE(669);
```

Controls subtitles shown over a movie. With mode 0, value turns captions on (1) or off (0), and off clears all 18 caption slots. With mode 1, value is the slot (0 to 17) and the caption shows text from start_frame for frames frames, both NTSC frames (30 a second), which the PAL engine takes 5/6 of.

Original name: `_SET_MOVIE_CC`.

### register_villager_here

```c
extern void register_villager_here(chara_slot chara, int villager_slot, enum memory_stack memory_stack) NATIVE(670);
```

Registers a scene character as a town villager in the given villager slot, using the character's current position and facing as its home spot. The spot record is allocated from the numbered scene memory stack.

Original name: `_REGISTER_VILLAGER2`.

### set_fishing_tournament

```c
extern void set_fishing_tournament(enum fish_tour_set_command command, OPTIONAL(int value)) NATIVE(671);
```

Runs a fishing tournament command. 0 sets the tournament rank to value (kept within 0 to 100), 1 clears the recorded entries, 2 loads the tournament prize list, 3 advances the tournament counter (wrapping back to 1).

Original name: `_SET_FISHINGTOURNAMENT_ETC`.

### bind_handle_chara

```c
extern void bind_handle_chara(int handle, int chara) NATIVE(700);
```

Binds an event object handle (0 to 31) to a scene character, so the other handle natives act on it.

Original name: `_EOH_SYNC_CHARA`.

### bind_handle_map_part

```c
extern void bind_handle_map_part(int handle, any part, OPTIONAL(string piece_name, string frame_name)) NATIVE(701);
```

Binds a handle to a placed map part of the active maps, found by number or by name. With a piece name it binds that piece of the part in local coordinates; with a frame name too it binds that frame of the piece.

Original name: `_EOH_SYNC_OBJ`.

### set_handle_pos

```c
extern void set_handle_pos(int handle, float x, float y, float z) NATIVE(702);
```

Moves the object bound to a handle to a position in world units, relative to the event's world origin when one is set. Sprites and frames take the position as given.

Original name: `_EOH_SET_POS`.

### set_handle_rot

```c
extern void set_handle_rot(int handle, float x, float y, OPTIONAL(float z)) NATIVE(703);
```

Sets the rotation of the object bound to a handle, in radians, offset by the event's world rotation for characters and world objects. Sprites use only z. The engine rejects calls with three arguments, so pass all of x, y and z.

Original name: `_EOH_SET_ROT`.

### get_handle_pos

```c
extern void get_handle_pos(int handle, float *x, float *y, float *z) NATIVE(704);
```

Writes the position of the object bound to a handle, in the event's coordinates, to x, y and z. They are left unchanged when the handle is empty.

Original name: `_EOH_GET_POS`.

### get_handle_rot

```c
extern void get_handle_rot(int handle, float *x, float *y, float *z) NATIVE(705);
```

Writes the rotation in radians of the object bound to a handle to x, y and z. They are left unchanged when the handle is empty.

Original name: `_EOH_GET_ROT`.

### set_handle_motion

```c
extern void set_handle_motion(int handle, string motion_name, OPTIONAL(enum chara_motion_flag flags, float step, int unused)) NATIVE(706);
```

Starts a named motion on the character bound to a handle. flags is a bit set: 1 pauses, 2 holds the last frame instead of looping, 4 restarts from the first frame. When step is given and not -1, the motion switches at once and plays at that many frames per tick. Calls with a fifth argument are rejected.

Original name: `_EOH_SET_MOTION`.

### set_handle_motion_step

```c
extern void set_handle_motion_step(int handle, float step) NATIVE(707);
```

Sets how many motion frames the character bound to a handle advances per tick.

Original name: `_EOH_SET_STEP`.

### set_handle_texture_anim

```c
extern void set_handle_texture_anim(int handle, any mode, OPTIONAL(string anim_name)) NATIVE(708);
```

Turns texture animations of the character bound to a handle on or off. (handle, 0) stops them all; (handle, on, name) turns the named one on or off; (handle, name) turns it on; (handle, off_name, on_name) switches from one to the other.

Original name: `_EOH_SET_TEX_ANIM`.

### set_handle_scale

```c
extern void set_handle_scale(int handle, float x, float y, float z) NATIVE(709);
```

Sets the scale of the object bound to a handle on each axis. 1.0 is normal size.

Original name: `_EOH_SET_SCALE`.

### set_handle_visible

```c
extern void set_handle_visible(int handle, int visible) NATIVE(710);
```

Shows (1) or hides (0) the character or map object bound to a handle. For a function point it enables or disables the point.

Original name: `_EOH_SET_SHOW`.

### get_handle_visible

```c
extern int get_handle_visible(int handle) NATIVE(711);
```

Returns whether the object bound to a handle is shown. The result is left unchanged when the handle is empty.

Original name: `_EOH_GET_SHOW`.

### set_handle_frame_visible

```c
extern void set_handle_frame_visible(int handle, string frame_name, int visible) NATIVE(712);
```

Shows or hides a named model frame of the character or frame bound to a handle. Any nonzero visible counts as 1.

Original name: `_EOH_SET_FRAME_SHOW`.

### set_handle_shadow

```c
extern void set_handle_shadow(int handle, int on) NATIVE(713);
```

Turns the shadow of the character bound to a handle on (1) or off (0).

Original name: `_EOH_SET_SHADOW`.

### set_handle_translate

```c
extern void set_handle_translate(int handle, float x, float y, float z) NATIVE(714);
```

Writes a translation into the root frame matrix of the character, or the local matrix of the frame, bound to a handle, offsetting the model from its position.

Original name: `_EOH_SET_TRANSLATE`.

### bind_handle_sprite

```c
extern void bind_handle_sprite(int handle, int sprite) NATIVE(715);
```

Binds a handle to an event sprite by number.

Original name: `_EOH_SYNC_SPRITE`.

### set_handle_foot_sound

```c
extern void set_handle_foot_sound(int handle, int foot_sound_id) NATIVE(716);
```

Sets the footstep sound id of the character bound to a handle.

Original name: `_EOH_SET_FOOT_SOUND_ID`.

### set_handle_frame_draw

```c
extern void set_handle_frame_draw(int handle, string frame_name, int draw) NATIVE(717);
```

Writes a raw draw value into a named model frame of the character or frame bound to a handle. 0 hides the frame.

Original name: `_EOH_SET_FRAME_STATUS`.

### get_handle_frame_pos

```c
extern void get_handle_frame_pos(int handle, string frame_name, float *x, float *y, float *z) NATIVE(718);
```

Writes the world position of a named model frame of the character bound to a handle, in the event's coordinates, to x, y and z. They are left unchanged when the frame is not found.

Original name: `_EOH_GET_FRAME_POS`.

### set_handle_se_bank

```c
extern void set_handle_se_bank(int handle, int sound_id) NATIVE(719);
```

Sets the sound effect bank id that the character bound to a handle plays its motion sounds from.

Original name: `_EOH_SET_SOUND_ID`.

### get_handle_frame_draw

```c
extern int get_handle_frame_draw(int handle, string frame_name) NATIVE(720);
```

Returns the draw value of a named model frame of the character or frame bound to a handle, or 0 when the frame is not found.

Original name: `_EOH_GET_FRAME_STATUS`.

### bind_handle_chara_frame

```c
extern void bind_handle_chara_frame(int handle, int chara, string frame_name) NATIVE(721);
```

Binds a handle to a named model frame inside a scene character, so it can be moved, rotated or hidden on its own.

Original name: `_EOH_SYNC_CHROBJ`.

### set_handle_fade_flag

```c
extern void set_handle_fade_flag(int handle, int flag) NATIVE(722);
```

Sets the fade flag of the character bound to a handle.

Original name: `_EOH_SET_FADE_FLAG`.

### reset_handle_dynamics

```c
extern void reset_handle_dynamics(int handle) NATIVE(723);
```

Updates the position of the character bound to a handle, resets its dynamic animation parts (swinging pieces such as hair or cloth) to rest, then steps them 10 times. Used after teleporting a character.

Original name: `_EOH_RESET_DA_POSITION`.

### set_handle_shadow_draw

```c
extern void set_handle_shadow_draw(int handle, string frame_name, int draw) NATIVE(724);
```

Writes a raw draw value into a named frame of the shadow model of the character bound to a handle. 0 hides it.

Original name: `_EOH_SET_SHADOW_FRAME_STATUS`.

### bind_handle_geostone

```c
extern void bind_handle_geostone(int handle) NATIVE(725);
```

Binds a handle to the dungeon geostone object.

Original name: `_EOH_SYNC_GEOSTONE`.

### bind_handle_named_chara

```c
extern void bind_handle_named_chara(int handle, int unused, string chara_name) NATIVE(726);
```

Binds a handle to the character with the given name among the player's linked characters. The second argument is ignored.

Original name: `_EOH_SYNC_SEARCH_CHARA`.

### apply_handle_motion

```c
extern void apply_handle_motion(int handle) NATIVE(727);
```

Applies the pending motion change of the character bound to a handle at once instead of waiting for the next update.

Original name: `_EOH_NORMAL_DRIVE`.

### bind_handle_edit_part

```c
extern void bind_handle_edit_part(int handle, any part, OPTIONAL(string piece_name, string frame_name)) NATIVE(728);
```

Binds a handle to a placed part of the active edit map, found by number or by name. With a piece name it binds that piece in local coordinates; with a frame name too it binds that frame of the piece.

Original name: `_EOH_SYNC_EDIT_OBJ`.

### set_handle_frame_alpha

```c
extern void set_handle_frame_alpha(int handle, string frame_name, float alpha) NATIVE(729);
```

Sets the opacity of a named model frame of the character or frame bound to a handle, from 0.0 (invisible) to 1.0.

Original name: `_EOH_SET_FRAME_ALPHA`.

### bind_handle_func_point

```c
extern void bind_handle_func_point(int handle, any part, string point_name) NATIVE(730);
```

Binds a handle to a named function point of the active map. part picks the placed part that owns it, by number or by name; an empty name "" searches the map's own points.

Original name: `_EOH_SYNC_FUNCP`.

### set_handle_foot_bank

```c
extern void set_handle_foot_bank(int handle, int bank) NATIVE(731);
```

Sets the footstep sound effect bank of the character bound to a handle.

Original name: `_EOH_SET_FOOT_SE_ID`.

### bind_handle_door

```c
extern int bind_handle_door(int handle) NATIVE(732);
```

Binds a handle to the door part of the generated dungeon floor and returns 1 on success, 0 otherwise.

Original name: `_EOH_SYNC_DOOR_PARTS`.

### init_sphida

```c
extern void init_sphida(void) NATIVE(750);
```

Clears the sphida (dungeon golf) game state so no game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_INIT`.

### create_sphida_game

```c
extern void create_sphida_game(enum memory_stack memory_stack, int tex_bank, OPTIONAL(int kind, int course)) NATIVE(751);
```

Creates a sphida game in the numbered scene memory stack and starts it. kind 0 places the pin and ball at random on the dungeon floor, 1 uses the fixed story hole, 2 sets up bonus course number course. tex_bank is the texture bank the status panel draws from.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_SET_UP`.

### set_sphida_playing

```c
extern void set_sphida_playing(int on) NATIVE(752);
```

Sets whether the sphida game runs; while 0 nothing is stepped or drawn. Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_SET_PLAY_FLAG`.

### set_sphida_map_scroll

```c
extern void set_sphida_map_scroll(int on) NATIVE(753);
```

Lets the player scroll the sphida mini map while an event runs (1) or not (0). Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_SET_MINIMAP_FLAG`.

### set_sphida_line_marks

```c
extern void set_sphida_line_marks(int on) NATIVE(754);
```

Shows (1) or hides (0) the shot line points on the sphida mini map. Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_SET_MM_LINE_FLAG`.

### set_sphida_line_point

```c
extern void set_sphida_line_point(int index, float x, float y, float z) NATIVE(755);
```

Sets one of the 5 shot line points (index 0 to 4) marked on the sphida mini map, in world units. Indexes of 5 or more are ignored. Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_SET_MM_LINE_POS`.

### set_sphida_pin_pos

```c
extern void set_sphida_pin_pos(float x, float y, float z) NATIVE(756);
```

Sets the position of the sphida pin in world units. Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_SET_PIN_POS`.

### get_sphida_pin_pos

```c
extern void get_sphida_pin_pos(float *x, float *y, float *z) NATIVE(757);
```

Writes the position of the sphida pin to x, y and z. Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_GET_PIN_POS`.

### set_sphida_ball_pos

```c
extern void set_sphida_ball_pos(float x, float y, float z) NATIVE(758);
```

Sets the position of the sphida ball in world units. Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_SET_BALL_POS`.

### get_sphida_ball_pos

```c
extern void get_sphida_ball_pos(float *x, float *y, float *z) NATIVE(759);
```

Writes the position of the sphida ball to x, y and z. Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_GET_BALL_POS`.

### set_sphida_pin_color

```c
extern void set_sphida_pin_color(int color) NATIVE(760);
```

Sets the pin colour, 0 or 1, which picks its mini map marker. Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_SET_PIN_COL`.

### get_sphida_pin_color

```c
extern int get_sphida_pin_color(void) NATIVE(761);
```

Returns the pin colour (0 or 1). Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_GET_PIN_COL`.

### set_sphida_ball_color

```c
extern void set_sphida_ball_color(int color) NATIVE(762);
```

Sets the ball colour, 0 or 1, which picks its mini map marker. Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_SET_BALL_COL`.

### get_sphida_ball_color

```c
extern int get_sphida_ball_color(void) NATIVE(763);
```

Returns the ball colour (0 or 1). Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_GET_BALL_COL`.

### set_sphida_par

```c
extern void set_sphida_par(int par) NATIVE(764);
```

Sets the shots of par left for the hole. Ignored while the debug flag is on. Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_SET_PAR_COUNT`.

### get_sphida_par

```c
extern int get_sphida_par(void) NATIVE(765);
```

Returns the shots of par left for the hole (one more while the debug flag is on). Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_GET_PAR_COUNT`.

### get_sphida_map_scale

```c
extern int get_sphida_map_scale(void) NATIVE(766);
```

Returns the sphida mini map scale (0 to 2, from the dungeon map setting). Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_GET_MINI_LEVEL`.

### get_sphida_tex_bank

```c
extern int get_sphida_tex_bank(void) NATIVE(767);
```

Returns the texture bank the sphida status panel draws from. Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_GET_TEXB`.

### set_sphida_status_panel

```c
extern void set_sphida_status_panel(int on) NATIVE(768);
```

Shows (1) or hides (0) the sphida status panel and power gauge. Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_SET_STATUS_FLAG`.

### reset_sphida_gauge

```c
extern void reset_sphida_gauge(void) NATIVE(769);
```

Resets the sphida power gauge to idle: no power, cursor at rest, result code -10. Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_RESET_POWGAGE`.

### start_sphida_gauge

```c
extern void start_sphida_gauge(void) NATIVE(770);
```

Starts a swing on the sphida power gauge; the cursor begins to charge. Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_START_POWGAGE`.

### press_sphida_gauge

```c
extern void press_sphida_gauge(void) NATIVE(771);
```

Presses the sphida power gauge: while charging it sets the power, while running back to the impact point it stops the cursor for judging. Does nothing at other stages. Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_TRIGGER_POWGAGE`.

### get_sphida_shot_power

```c
extern float get_sphida_shot_power(void) NATIVE(772);
```

Returns the power set on the sphida gauge, from 0.0 to 1.0. Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_GET_SHOT_POW`.

### get_sphida_gauge_result

```c
extern int get_sphida_gauge_result(void) NATIVE(773);
```

Returns the result of the last swing: -10 no swing yet, -3 to 3 how far from the impact point the cursor stopped (0 is on it), 4 ran past without a stop, 5 ran back to empty without power set. Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_GET_POWGAGE_CODE`.

### set_sphida_safe_zone

```c
extern void set_sphida_safe_zone(int level) NATIVE(774);
```

Sets the width of the accurate zone around the impact point on the sphida gauge, kept within 1 to 6. Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_SET_POWGAGE_SAFE_LEVEL`.

### get_sphida_club_stats

```c
extern void get_sphida_club_stats(int club_no, float *power, float *extra, void *safe_level) NATIVE(775);
```

Writes the stats of sphida club number 9 to 14: power is the launch speed, extra is a second float the shot scripts read, and safe_level is the gauge safe zone width. Fails for other numbers.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_GET_CULB_DEF`.

### set_sphida_spin_mark

```c
extern void set_sphida_spin_mark(float x, float y) NATIVE(776);
```

Sets the spin mark on the sphida ball panel, x across and y down, each from -1.0 to 1.0. Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_SET_SPIN_MARK_POS`.

### set_sphida_club

```c
extern void set_sphida_club(int club_no) NATIVE(777);
```

Sets the club the player holds in the sphida game (9 to 14). Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_SET_CULB_NO`.

### set_sphida_carry_angle

```c
extern void set_sphida_carry_angle(float angle) NATIVE(778);
```

Stores the angle in radians at which the sphida ball is struck, which the status panel uses to draw the carry. Nothing is calculated here. Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_CALC_CARRY`.

### get_sphida_cursor_pos

```c
extern void get_sphida_cursor_pos(float *x, float *y) NATIVE(779);
```

Writes the screen position of the sphida gauge cursor to x and y. Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_GET_PG_CURSOR_POS`.

### load_sphida_pin_collision

```c
extern void load_sphida_pin_collision(enum memory_stack memory_stack, string model_file) NATIVE(780);
```

Loads the pin collision from a model file that must already be in the preloaded background buffers, allocating from the numbered scene memory stack. Fails when no sphida game exists.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_SET_COL_MODEL`.

### get_sphida_prize

```c
extern void get_sphida_prize(int index, int *item, int *count) NATIVE(781);
```

Writes the sphida prize item and its count for the current dungeon floor to item and count. index picks one of the floor's 3 prizes; values above 2 use the last.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_GET_PRIZE`.

### set_sphida_last_challenge

```c
extern void set_sphida_last_challenge(int on) NATIVE(782);
```

Marks whether the last challenge of the current spheda hole is under way (nonzero) or not (0). Fails while no spheda game is running.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_SET_LAST_CHALLENGE`.

### get_sphida_last_challenge

```c
extern int get_sphida_last_challenge(void) NATIVE(783);
```

Returns the spheda last-challenge flag set by set_sphida_last_challenge. Fails while no spheda game is running.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_GET_LAST_CHALLENGE`.

### get_sphida_bonus_mode

```c
extern int get_sphida_bonus_mode(void) NATIVE(784);
```

Returns 1 while one of the spheda bonus courses is being played, else 0. Fails while no spheda game is running.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_GET_OMAKE_MODE`.

### set_sphida_hole

```c
extern void set_sphida_hole(int hole) NATIVE(785);
```

Sets the current spheda hole number in the save data.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_SET_NOW_HOLE`.

### get_sphida_hole

```c
extern int get_sphida_hole(void) NATIVE(786);
```

Returns the current spheda hole number from the save data.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_GET_NOW_HOLE`.

### set_sphida_score

```c
extern void set_sphida_score(int hole, int score) NATIVE(787);
```

Stores a spheda score for hole 0 to 8 in the save data; hole = -1 means the current hole. Out- of-range holes are ignored.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_SET_SCORE`.

### get_sphida_score

```c
extern int get_sphida_score(int hole) NATIVE(788);
```

Returns the saved spheda score of hole 0 to 8. With hole = -1 it returns the total of all nine holes; holes above 8 give 0.

Original name: `_SPHIDA_GET_SCORE`.

### run_mt_test

```c
extern void run_mt_test(void) NATIVE(998);
```

Debug leftover that does nothing.

Original name: `_MT_TEST`.

### run_test

```c
extern void run_test(OPTIONAL(any value)) NATIVE(999);
```

Debug leftover that does nothing and ignores its argument.

Original name: `_TEST`.

### set_vector_zero

```c
extern void set_vector_zero(float *x, float *y, float *z) NATIVE(1000);
```

Sets the three out variables to 0.0.

Original name: `_ZERO_VECTOR`.

### normalize_vector

```c
extern void normalize_vector(float *x, float *y, float *z) NATIVE(1001);
```

Normalizes the vector held in x, y, z in place to length 1.

Original name: `_NORMAL_VECTOR`.

### copy_vector

```c
extern void copy_vector(float *x, float *y, float *z, float src_x, float src_y, float src_z) NATIVE(1002);
```

Copies src_x, src_y, src_z into the three out variables.

Original name: `_COPY_VECTOR`.

### add_vector

```c
extern void add_vector(float *x, float *y, float *z, float add_x, float add_y, float add_z) NATIVE(1003);
```

Adds add_x, add_y, add_z to the vector held in x, y, z, in place.

Original name: `_ADD_VECTOR`.

### subtract_vector

```c
extern void subtract_vector(float *x, float *y, float *z, float sub_x, float sub_y, float sub_z) NATIVE(1004);
```

Subtracts sub_x, sub_y, sub_z from the vector held in x, y, z, in place.

Original name: `_SUB_VECTOR`.

### scale_vector

```c
extern void scale_vector(float *x, float *y, float *z, float scale) NATIVE(1005);
```

Multiplies the vector held in x, y, z by scale, in place.

Original name: `_SCALE_VECTOR`.

### divide_vector

```c
extern void divide_vector(float *x, float *y, float *z, float divisor) NATIVE(1006);
```

Divides the vector held in x, y, z by divisor, in place. Fails and leaves the vector unchanged when divisor is 0.

Original name: `_DIV_VECTOR`.

### get_vector_length

```c
extern float get_vector_length(float x, float y, float z) NATIVE(1007);
```

Returns the length of the vector x, y, z. Pass 0.0 for y to get the length on the ground plane.

Original name: `_DIST_VECTOR`.

### get_distance

```c
extern float get_distance(float x1, float y1, float z1, float x2, float y2, float z2) NATIVE(1008);
```

Returns the distance between two points, in world units.

Original name: `_DIST_VECTOR2`.

### get_square_root

```c
extern float get_square_root(float value) NATIVE(1009);
```

Returns the square root of value.

Original name: `_SQRT`.

### get_atan2

```c
extern float get_atan2(float y, float x) NATIVE(1010);
```

Returns atan2(y, x) in radians from -pi to pi. Scripts pass x then z of an offset to get a yaw angle.

Original name: `_ATAN2F`.

### compare_angles

```c
extern int compare_angles(float a, float b, float tolerance) NATIVE(1011);
```

Compares two angles in radians, wrapping their difference into -pi..pi. Returns 0 when a - b is within tolerance, 1 when it is larger and -1 when it is smaller.

Original name: `_ANGLE_CMP`.

### wrap_angle

```c
extern void wrap_angle(float *angle) NATIVE(1012);
```

Wraps the angle held in the variable into the range -pi..pi radians, in place.

Original name: `_ANGLE_LIMIT`.

### get_random

```c
extern void get_random(any range, void *result) NATIVE(1013);
```

Reads a random number from 0 up to but not including range into result. A float range gives a float; an int range gives an int.

Original name: `_GET_RAND`.

### get_segment_point_dist

```c
extern float get_segment_point_dist(float ax, float ay, float az, float bx, float by, float bz, float px, float py, float pz) NATIVE(1014);
```

Returns the distance from point p to the segment from a to b, or -1.0 when p projects outside the segment.

Original name: `_LINE_POINT_DIST`.

### create_sword_trail

```c
extern void create_sword_trail(enum memory_stack stack_no, int capacity, int division) NATIVE(1015);
```

Creates the single event sword trail, a ribbon drawn between pairs of points, in the given scene memory stack. capacity is how many point pairs it keeps and division how many smoothed points are made between two pairs; both colours start at 128, 128, 128, 128.

Original name: `_CREATE_SWORD_EFFECT`.

### delete_sword_trail

```c
extern void delete_sword_trail(void) NATIVE(1016);
```

Forgets the event sword trail made by create_sword_trail so it is no longer drawn. Its memory stays in the scene stack.

Original name: `_DELETE_SWORD_EFFECT`.

### set_sword_trail_color

```c
extern void set_sword_trail_color(int edge_r, int edge_g, int edge_b, int edge_a, int back_r, int back_g, int back_b, int back_a) NATIVE(1017);
```

Sets the RGBA colour of the leading edge and of the trailing edge of the event sword trail, each 0 to 255 with 128 as neutral. Fails if no trail exists.

Original name: `_SWORD_EFFECT_COLOR`.

### add_sword_trail_point

```c
extern void add_sword_trail_point(float edge_x, float edge_y, float edge_z, float back_x, float back_y, float back_z, OPTIONAL(float life)) NATIVE(1018);
```

Records a new pair of points, the leading edge and the trailing edge, into the event sword trail. life defaults to 1.0 and drops by 0.1 each frame until the pair disappears. Does nothing if no trail exists.

Original name: `_SWORD_EFFECT_ADD_POINT`.

### add_chara_pos

```c
extern void add_chara_pos(chara_slot chara, float dx, float dy, float dz) NATIVE(1019);
```

Moves a character by an offset added to its current position, in world units.

Original name: `_ADD_CHARA_POS`.

### add_chara_rot

```c
extern void add_chara_rot(chara_slot chara, float rx, float ry, float rz) NATIVE(1020);
```

Turns a character by adding to its current rotation, in radians. Each axis is wrapped into -pi..pi.

Original name: `_ADD_CHARA_ROT`.

### spawn_treasure_box

```c
extern void spawn_treasure_box(float x, float y, float z, float angle, item_id item, OPTIONAL(int count)) NATIVE(1021);
```

Puts a treasure box in the first free slot of the dungeon at x, y, z, turned by angle radians, holding count of item number item (count defaults to 1).

Original name: `_POST_TREASURE_BOX`.

### get_nearest_parts_pos

```c
extern void get_nearest_parts_pos(float x, float y, float z, float radius, float *parts_x, float *parts_y, float *parts_z) NATIVE(1022);
```

Finds the map parts placed within a box of half-size radius around x, y, z and reads the position of the nearest one into the out variables. Fails without writing when no parts are found.

Original name: `_GET_PARTS_ORIGIN`.

### step_control_camera

```c
extern void step_control_camera(void) NATIVE(1023);
```

Advances the player-controlled camera by one frame.

Original name: `_CTRLC_STEP`.

### set_control_camera_angle

```c
extern void set_control_camera_angle(float angle) NATIVE(1024);
```

Puts the player-controlled camera's eye at an angle around its look-at point, in radians, keeping its distance and height.

Original name: `_CTRLC_SET_ROTATE`.

### move_control_camera

```c
extern void move_control_camera(int chara) NATIVE(1025);
```

Moves the player-controlled camera from the pad's turn and swing-back input, using the character's rotation and keeping the camera clear of map walls near its look-at point. Fails if the character does not exist.

Original name: `_CTRLC_MOVE_CAMERA`.

### set_camera_rot_cancel

```c
extern void set_camera_rot_cancel(enum camera_rot_cancel mask) NATIVE(1026);
```

Sets which ways the player-controlled camera may not move, as bits: 1 shoulder buttons do not turn it, 2 right stick does not turn it, 0x40 the swing-behind button does nothing, 0x80 it does not swing round walls. 0 allows all.

Original name: `_CTRLC_SET_ROT_CANCEL`.

### set_control_camera_range

```c
extern void set_control_camera_range(float near_height, float far_height, float min_dist, float max_dist) NATIVE(1027);
```

Sets the limits of the player-controlled camera: height added to the eye at the nearest and farthest distance, and the minimum and maximum horizontal distance from the look-at point, in world units.

Original name: `_CTRLC_MOVE_RANGE`.

### get_nearest_treasure_box

```c
extern void get_nearest_treasure_box(float x, float y, float z, float *box_x, float *box_y, float *box_z, int *index) NATIVE(1028);
```

Finds the nearest active treasure box of the dungeon within 30 units of x, y, z. Reads its position into box_x, box_y, box_z and its slot into index, or 0, 0, 0 and -1 when none is near.

Original name: `_GET_NEAR_TBOX_POS`.

### convert_chara_no

```c
extern int convert_chara_no(int short_no) NATIVE(1029);
```

Stub left in the engine: it returns nothing and always reports failure.

Original name: `_CONV_CHRNO_S2L`.

### init_chara_trail

```c
extern void init_chara_trail(chara_slot chara, int slot, enum memory_stack stack_no, int capacity, int division) NATIVE(1030);
```

Creates a weapon trail in trail slot 0 or 1 of a character, allocated in the given scene memory stack. capacity is how many recorded points it keeps and division how many smoothed points are made between two; both colours start at 128, 128, 128, 128.

Original name: `_SWE_INIT`.

### set_chara_trail_color

```c
extern void set_chara_trail_color(chara_slot chara, int slot, int r0, int g0, int b0, int a0, int r1, int g1, int b1, int a1) NATIVE(1031);
```

Sets the RGBA colours of the two edges of a character's weapon trail, each 0 to 255 with 128 as neutral. Fails if the trail slot is empty.

Original name: `_SWE_SET_COLOR`.

### set_chara_trail_texture

```c
extern void set_chara_trail_texture(chara_slot chara, int slot, int tex_block, string tex_name, int u, int v, int width, int height) NATIVE(1032);
```

Maps a character's weapon trail with the named texture from texture block tex_block, using the texel rectangle starting at u, v with the given width and height. Fails if the trail slot is empty or the texture is not found.

Original name: `_SWE_SET_TEXTURE`.

### start_chara_trail

```c
extern void start_chara_trail(chara_slot chara, int slot, string from_frame, string to_frame, int length, int fade_frames, int hold_frames) NATIVE(1033);
```

Starts a character's weapon trail between two named model frames, such as the blade's base and tip. length is how many smoothed points stay fully opaque, hold_frames how long it stays before fading and fade_frames how long the fade takes.

Original name: `_SWE_START_EFFECT`.

### set_chara_scene_type

```c
extern void set_chara_scene_type(chara_slot chara, enum scene_chara_type type) NATIVE(1034);
```

Sets the scene type of a character slot. Type 1 is hit by attacks aimed at the player, 3 by attacks aimed at monsters, and 4 is drawn in the later effect pass after the other characters.

Original name: `_SET_CHARA_TYPE`.

### get_event_data

```c
extern void get_event_data(enum event_data_field field, void *value, OPTIONAL(void *value2, void *value3, void *value4)) NATIVE(1035);
```

Reads a field of the event that started the script into the out variables. field: 0 flag, 1 event number, 2 point number, 3-5 event args 1-3, 6 map event check type, 7 map event number, 8 position x, y, z and yaw, 9 map parts number, 10 position x, y, z, 11 rotation (y only with one out, x, y, z with three), 12 scale x, y, z, 13 character slot, 14 character number, 15 game object number.

Original name: `_GET_EVENT_DATA`.

### set_prev_floor

```c
extern void set_prev_floor(int floor) NATIVE(1036);
```

Stores the previous floor number of the current dungeon in the save data.

Original name: `_DNG_SET_PREV_FLOOR`.

### get_prev_floor

```c
extern int get_prev_floor(void) NATIVE(1037);
```

Returns the previous floor number of the current dungeon from the save data.

Original name: `_DNG_GET_PREV_FLOOR`.

### set_fast_floor

```c
extern void set_fast_floor(void) NATIVE(1038);
```

Stub left in the engine: it does nothing and always reports failure.

Original name: `_DNG_SET_FAST_FLOOR`.

### set_floor_info

```c
extern void set_floor_info(int dungeon, int floor, enum floor_info_field field, any value) NATIVE(1039);
```

Writes one field of a dungeon floor's save record. field: 0 unknown, 1 best clear time in frames, 2 and 3 unknown, 4 spheda cleared, 5 progress bits ORed into the flags (1 open, 8 practice, 0x10 time clear, 0x20 fishing, 0x80 spheda, 0x100 geostone found, 0x200 geostone read, 0x400 seal cleared), 6 kill count, 7 visit count.

Original name: `_SET_FLOOR_INFO`.

### get_floor_info

```c
extern int get_floor_info(int dungeon, int floor, enum floor_info_field field) NATIVE(1040);
```

Returns one field of a dungeon floor's save record, using the same field numbers as set_floor_info; field 5 gives all the progress flag bits.

Original name: `_GET_FLOOR_INFO`.

### get_next_floor

```c
extern int get_next_floor(int floor, int route) NATIVE(1041);
```

Returns the floor that follows floor along the given route of the current dungeon map, or 0 when the floor is unknown.

Original name: `_GET_NEXT_FLOOR`.

### disable_pad_auto_repeat

```c
extern void disable_pad_auto_repeat(void) NATIVE(1042);
```

Turns off auto-repeat for held controller buttons.

Original name: `_PAD_AUTO_REPEAT_OFF`.

### set_pad_auto_repeat

```c
extern void set_pad_auto_repeat(enum pad_button mask, int delay, int interval) NATIVE(1043);
```

Turns on auto-repeat for the controller buttons in mask, firing after delay frames and then every interval frames while held.

Original name: `_PAD_SET_AUTO_REPEAT`.

### set_dungeon_pause

```c
extern void set_dungeon_pause(enum dng_pause mask, int on) NATIVE(1044);
```

Sets (on = 1) or clears (on = 0) dungeon pause bits. Bits: 1 monster AI, 2 player step, 4 player control, 0x10 monster draw, 0x20 sky draw, 0x80 map draw, 0x100 minimap, 0x200 pickups, 0x400 player status, 0x800 exit heal, 0x1000 enemy step, 0x2000 weapon draw, 0x4000 battle music, 0x8000 pad reset, 0x10000 monster names.

Original name: `_DNG_PAUSE`.

### check_dungeon_pause

```c
extern int check_dungeon_pause(enum dng_pause mask) NATIVE(1045);
```

Returns the dungeon pause bits that are set within mask; nonzero means at least one is set.

Original name: `_DNG_CHECK_PAUSE`.

### reset_dungeon_timer

```c
extern void reset_dungeon_timer(void) NATIVE(1046);
```

Resets the dungeon frame timer to 0.

Original name: `_DNG_RESET_TIMER`.

### get_dungeon_timer

```c
extern int get_dungeon_timer(void) NATIVE(1047);
```

Returns the number of frames counted since the dungeon timer was last reset.

Original name: `_DNG_GET_TIMER`.

### load_chara_skin

```c
extern void load_chara_skin(enum memory_stack stack_no, any pack, string info_name, int chara) NATIVE(1048);
```

Loads a skin onto a character from a pack file into scene memory stack stack_no. pack is either an item number, whose model file is used, or a file path string; info_name names the skin entry inside the pack.

Original name: `_LOAD_SKIN`.

### check_camera_collision

```c
extern void check_camera_collision(float from_x, float from_y, float from_z, float to_x, float to_y, float to_z, void *hit_or_hit_x, OPTIONAL(float *hit_y, float *hit_z, int *hit)) NATIVE(1049);
```

Tests the line from one point to another against the map's camera collision. With 7 arguments the 7th receives the index of the polygon hit or -1; with 10 arguments the 7th to 9th receive the hit position and the 10th the polygon index.

Original name: `_CHK_CAMERA_COL`.

### get_parts_point_pos

```c
extern void get_parts_point_pos(float x, float y, float z, float radius, string point_name, int *found, float *point_x, float *point_y, float *point_z) NATIVE(1050);
```

Finds the map parts nearest x, y, z within radius and looks up the named function point on it. found receives 1 and the point's world position goes into the out variables, or found receives 0 when the parts has no such point; fails without writing if no parts are near.

Original name: `_GET_PARTS_FUNC_POS`.

### get_random_circle_pos

```c
extern void get_random_circle_pos(int circle, float *x, float *y, float *z) NATIVE(1051);
```

Reads the world position of random circle 0 to 2 of the floor into the out variables; circle = -1 means the circle the player last stood on. Fails without writing when there is none.

Original name: `_RANDOM_CIRCLE_GET_POS`.

### disable_random_circle

```c
extern void disable_random_circle(int circle) NATIVE(1052);
```

Turns off random circle 0 to 2 of the floor; circle = -1 means the circle the player last stood on.

Original name: `_RANDOM_CIRCLE_OFF`.

### swap_map_lighting

```c
extern void swap_map_lighting(void) NATIVE(1053);
```

Swaps the first eight lighting entries of the active map with the next eight, if the map has at least sixteen.

Original name: `_DNG_XCHG_MAP_LIGHT`.

### stop_geostone_anim

```c
extern void stop_geostone_anim(void) NATIVE(1054);
```

Stops the geostone floating up and down.

Original name: `_GEOSTONE_ANIME_OFF`.

### set_geostone_flag

```c
extern void set_geostone_flag(int on) NATIVE(1055);
```

Sets whether the geostone is on the floor. With 0 the geostone's map parts are moved away to a fixed hidden position.

Original name: `_GEOSTONE_SET_FLAG`.

### attach_geostone

```c
extern void attach_geostone(chara_slot chara, string frame_name) NATIVE(1056);
```

Attaches the geostone model to a named frame of a character's model so it follows it, such as "item" for a held item.

Original name: `_GEOSTONE_SET_REFERENCE`.

### detach_geostone

```c
extern void detach_geostone(void) NATIVE(1057);
```

Detaches the geostone model from the character frame set by attach_geostone.

Original name: `_GEOSTONE_DEL_REFERENCE`.

### get_player_move_type

```c
extern int get_player_move_type(void) NATIVE(1058);
```

Returns how the player character moves: 0 on foot, 1 or 4 ridepod on legs, 2 or 5 on treads, 3 on wheels, 6 through the air.

Original name: `_GET_ROBO_MOVE_TYPE`.

### set_exit_flag

```c
extern void set_exit_flag(int value) NATIVE(1059);
```

Sets the scene's exit flag, which event scripts read back with get_exit_flag.

Original name: `_SET_EXIT_FLAG`.

### get_exit_flag

```c
extern int get_exit_flag(void) NATIVE(1060);
```

Returns the scene's exit flag.

Original name: `_GET_EXIT_FLAG`.

### get_e3_version

```c
extern int get_e3_version(void) NATIVE(1061);
```

Returns whether this is the E3 show demo build. The retail game always gives 0.

Original name: `_GET_E3_VERSION`.

### check_pad_button

```c
extern int check_pad_button(int button) NATIVE(1062);
```

Returns the state of logical pad button 0 to 127 this frame; nonzero when the button fires according to its binding.

Original name: `_CHK_PAD_CTRL`.

### stop_control_camera

```c
extern void stop_control_camera(void) NATIVE(1063);
```

Stops the player-controlled camera from moving.

Original name: `_CTRLC_STAY`.

### set_dungeon_brightness

```c
extern void set_dungeon_brightness(float rate) NATIVE(1064);
```

Sets the scale applied to the colours the dungeon is drawn with; 1.0 is normal.

Original name: `_BSCN_SET_BLIGHT_RATE`.

### get_random_circle_trap

```c
extern int get_random_circle_trap(int kind) NATIVE(1065);
```

Rolls and returns a random trap number for a random circle of kind 0 or 1. Gives -2 when the active character is 2, and -1 for character 3 unless the trap is 4 or 12.

Original name: `_GET_RND_CIRCLE_TRAPID`.

### apply_random_circle_trap

```c
extern float apply_random_circle_trap(int trap) NATIVE(1066);
```

Applies the effect of a random circle trap to the active character. Trap 1 returns 10% of the sum of both weapons' maximum ABS; 2 refills HP, 3 repairs both characters' weapons, 6 takes half the HP, 8 to 11 halve a weapon's durability. The result is only written for trap 1.

Original name: `_SET_RND_CIRCLE_STATUS`.

### show_status_bar

```c
extern void show_status_bar(int show, OPTIONAL(float speed)) NATIVE(1067);
```

Slides the dungeon status bar in (show = 1) or out (show = 0). speed is the fraction moved per frame, default 0.02 and scaled by 1.2; a speed of 1.0 or more shows or hides it at once.

Original name: `_SET_STATUSBAR_SHOW`.

### spawn_pull_items

```c
extern void spawn_pull_items(float x, float y, float z, enum pull_item_type type, int count, float value) NATIVE(1068);
```

Scatters count pickups from x, y, z that fly to the player. type: 0 coin and 3 large coin (value is the money), 1 weapon growth orb (value is the growth for the active character's weapon), 2 gate key, 4 item.

Original name: `_SET_PULL_ITEM`.

### open_chara_change_menu

```c
extern void open_chara_change_menu(int unused) NATIVE(1069);
```

Opens the dungeon character change menu; the script is suspended while it is open. The argument is read and ignored.

Original name: `_MENU_CHARA_CHENGE`.

### get_sound_port_bank

```c
extern int get_sound_port_bank(enum snd_port port) NATIVE(1070);
```

Returns the sound bank handle loaded into sound port 0 to 11.

Original name: `_GET_EVENT_INFO_SNDID`.

### get_parts_pos

```c
extern void get_parts_pos(any parts, float *x, float *y, float *z) NATIVE(1071);
```

Finds map parts in the active maps by number or by name and reads its position into the out variables. Fails if none is found.

Original name: `_GET_PARTS_POS`.

### disable_scene_skip

```c
extern void disable_scene_skip(void) NATIVE(1072);
```

Makes the running drama scene unskippable by clearing its skip state.

Original name: `_CANCEL_DRAMA_SCENE`.

### get_circle_motion_frame

```c
extern float get_circle_motion_frame(void) NATIVE(1073);
```

Returns the current motion frame of the random circle model.

Original name: `_GET_RNDC_MOT_NOWT`.

### set_chara_motion_frame

```c
extern void set_chara_motion_frame(chara_slot chara, float frame) NATIVE(1074);
```

Sets the current motion frame of a character, such as to sync it with get_circle_motion_frame.

Original name: `_SET_CHARA_MOT_NOWT`.

### update_chara_motion

```c
extern void update_chara_motion(chara_slot chara) NATIVE(1075);
```

Advances a character's motion one step: changes to its pending motion, blends into it and poses the model.

Original name: `_CHARA_NORMAL_DRIVE`.

### reset_chara_cloth

```c
extern void reset_chara_cloth(chara_slot chara) NATIVE(1076);
```

Puts a character's cloth and hair back to rest on its model, then simulates them for 10 steps so they settle.

Original name: `_CHARA_RESET_DA`.

### change_player_chara

```c
extern void change_player_chara(enum user_chara chara) NATIVE(1077);
```

Switches the dungeon player to another character, reloading its model, icons and sounds. Fails if that character is already active.

Original name: `_DNG_SETUP_MAIN_UNIT`.

### set_party_member

```c
extern void set_party_member(enum user_chara chara, any state) NATIVE(1078);
```

With an int, adds the character to the party when state is 1 and removes it otherwise. Passing &variable instead reads 1 into it if the character is in the party, else 0.

Original name: `_JOIN_PARTY_MEMBER`.

### set_chara_change_enabled

```c
extern void set_chara_change_enabled(enum user_chara chara, int on) NATIVE(1079);
```

Lets the player change to a character (on = 1) or forbids it (on = 0).

Original name: `_SET_CHARA_CHANGE_FLAG`.

### set_chara_change_mask

```c
extern void set_chara_change_mask(enum user_chara chara, int on) NATIVE(1080);
```

Unmasks (on = 1) or masks (on = 0) changing to a character, a second switch kept apart from set_chara_change_enabled.

Original name: `_SET_CHARA_CHANGE_MASK`.

### change_dir

```c
extern void change_dir(OPTIONAL(directory dir)) NATIVE(1081);
```

Sets the directory later file names are loaded relative to, such as "event/dng/". An empty string, or no argument, clears it so files load from the root.

Original name: `_CHANGE_DIR`.

### remove_item

```c
extern void remove_item(item_id item, OPTIONAL(int count)) NATIVE(1082);
```

Removes count of an item from the player's inventory; count defaults to 1.

Original name: `_SUB_ITEM`.

### equip_chara_item

```c
extern void equip_chara_item(enum user_chara chara, item_id item) NATIVE(1083);
```

Equips an owned item on character 0 to 2, swapping it with the item in its slot. Fails if the item is not owned or is already equipped. Only the equipment slots its item type has (weapons, clothes, ridepod parts); never an active-item slot, so not the camera.

Original name: `_SET_CHARA_EQUIP`.

### load_pack_file

```c
extern void load_pack_file(string file_name) NATIVE(1084);
```

Loads a pack file into the read buffer so later file loads search it first.

Original name: `_LOAD_PACK_FILE`.

### set_bit_ctrl

```c
extern void set_bit_ctrl(enum bit_ctrl bits, int on) NATIVE(1085);
```
