Sets (on = 1) or clears (otherwise) the given bits of the 8-bit control value kept in the save data.

Original name: `_SET_BIT_CTRL`.

### check_bit_ctrl

```c
extern int check_bit_ctrl(enum bit_ctrl bits) NATIVE(1086);
```

Returns 1 if any of the given bits of the save data's control value are set, else 0.

Original name: `_GET_BIT_CTRL`.

### load_arg_script

```c
extern void load_arg_script(enum memory_stack stack_no, string file_name) NATIVE(1087);
```

Loads a compiled argument script (.stb) and runs it at once to build the event argument data, kept in scene memory stack stack_no.

Original name: `_LOAD_ARG`.

### get_item_have_count

```c
extern int get_item_have_count(item_id item) NATIVE(1088);
```

Returns how many of an item the player owns.

Original name: `_GET_ITEM_HAVE_NUM`.

### set_skip_button

```c
extern void set_skip_button(int button) NATIVE(1089);
```

Sets the logical pad button that skips the drama scene.

Original name: `_SET_SKIP_BOTTON`.

### set_skip_fade_color

```c
extern void set_skip_fade_color(float r, float g, float b) NATIVE(1090);
```

Sets the colour the screen fades to when the drama scene is skipped, as red, green and blue from 0 to 255.

Original name: `_SET_SKIP_FCOL`.

### get_debug_mode

```c
extern int get_debug_mode(void) NATIVE(1091);
```

Returns 1. This build always reports debug mode on; scripts on the disc never call it.

Original name: `_GET_DEBUG_MODE`.

### get_map_type

```c
extern int get_map_type(any map, ...) NATIVE(1092);
```

Looks up a map by number or by name and returns its type: 1 for a georama map that has edit data, -1 when the map is not found.

Original name: `_GET_MAP_TYPE`.

### clear_dungeon_collisions

```c
extern void clear_dungeon_collisions(void) NATIVE(1093);
```

Reinitializes the dungeon scene's collision primitive manager, removing every collision primitive it holds.

Original name: `_DNG_COLLISION_ALL_CLR`.

### set_map_draw

```c
extern void set_map_draw(int on) NATIVE(1094);
```

Turns drawing of the map behind the event on (1) or off (0).

Original name: `_SET_MAP_DRAW`.

### check_memory_card_load

```c
extern int check_memory_card_load(void) NATIVE(1095);
```

Returns the memory card load state the current game mode was entered with (1 after loading a save from the title screen), then resets it to 0, so only the first call sees it.

Original name: `_CHECK_MC_LOAD`.

### set_current_map

```c
extern void set_current_map(map_id map_no) NATIVE(1096);
```

Sets the scene's current map number. When it changes, the old number is kept as the previous map.

Original name: `_SET_NOW_MAP_NO`.

### get_chest_contents

```c
extern void get_chest_contents(int *flags, int *item_a, int *item_b, int *count_a, int *count_b) NATIVE(1097);
```

Reads the treasure chest nearest the player in the dungeon: its flags, the two item numbers it holds and the count of each.

Original name: `_GET_TBOX_PARAM`.

### skip_villager_load

```c
extern void skip_villager_load(void) NATIVE(1098);
```

Makes the scene skip its next load of town villagers and sub villagers. Each skip flag clears itself once used.

Original name: `_CANCEL_LOAD_VILLAGER`.

### skip_loading_screen

```c
extern void skip_loading_screen(void) NATIVE(1099);
```

Sets a flag so the next request to start the now-loading screen returns at once without showing it.

Original name: `_CANCEL_NOW_LOADING`.

### init_effect_scripts

```c
extern void init_effect_scripts(enum memory_stack stack_no, OPTIONAL(int texb_offset)) NATIVE(1100);
```

Creates the event effect script manager in scene memory stack stack_no. It uses the event's texture blocks starting texb_offset blocks in (default 0). Call before any other effect script native.

Original name: `_ESM_INITIALIZE`.

### set_effect_script_heap

```c
extern void set_effect_script_heap(enum memory_stack stack_no, OPTIONAL(int heap_size)) NATIVE(1101);
```

Gives the effect script manager a work heap of heap_size bytes (default 14000) taken from scene memory stack stack_no. Needs init_effect_scripts first.

Original name: `_ESM_INIT_FIX`.

### clear_effect_scripts

```c
extern void clear_effect_scripts(void) NATIVE(1102);
```

Deletes every running effect, unloads every effect script base, empties the work heap, frees their texture blocks and removes the effect script manager.

Original name: `_ESM_CLEAR`.

### load_effect_script

```c
extern void load_effect_script(any effect) NATIVE(1103);
```

Loads an effect script base, given by name or by number, so effects can be created from it. Does nothing if it is already loaded.

Original name: `_ESM_LOAD_BASE`.

### create_effect

```c
extern void create_effect(string effect_name, OPTIONAL(int owner, int *slot)) NATIVE(1104);
```

Starts an effect from a loaded effect script base. owner (default -1) is the id the effect belongs to. With the third argument the effect also takes a free slot under owner and slot receives its number, or -1 on failure; later natives use owner and slot to address it. Natives without owner and slot act on the effect created last.

Original name: `_ESM_CREATE`.

### finish_effect

```c
extern void finish_effect(int owner, int slot) NATIVE(1105);
```

Switches the effect in owner's slot to program number 300, the program effect scripts use for their ending.

Original name: `_ESM_FINISH`.

### delete_effect

```c
extern void delete_effect(int owner, int slot) NATIVE(1106);
```

Deletes the effect in owner's slot at once.

Original name: `_ESM_DELETE`.

### set_effect_vector1

```c
extern void set_effect_vector1(float x, float y, float z) NATIVE(1107);
```

Sets the first work vector of the effect created last, usually its position in world units. The engine also accepts a five argument form (owner, slot, x, y, z) for an effect in a slot.

Original name: `_ESM_SET_VECT1`.

### set_effect_vector2

```c
extern void set_effect_vector2(float x, float y, float z) NATIVE(1108);
```

Sets the second work vector of the effect created last, such as a scale or direction, depending on the effect script. The engine also accepts a five argument form (owner, slot, x, y, z) for an effect in a slot.

Original name: `_ESM_SET_VECT2`.

### set_effect_target

```c
extern void set_effect_target(int target_id) NATIVE(1109);
```

Sets the target id of the effect created last; -1 for none. The engine also accepts a three argument form (owner, slot, target_id) for an effect in a slot.

Original name: `_ESM_SET_TARGET_ID`.

### load_effect_script_pack

```c
extern void load_effect_script_pack(string effect_name, string file_name) NATIVE(1110);
```

Builds the effect script base effect_name from file_name, which must already be loaded by the background loader or present in the loaded pack.

Original name: `_ESM_LOAD_BASE_PACK`.

### set_effect_value

```c
extern void set_effect_value(int index, any value) NATIVE(1111);
```

Sets work value number index of the effect created last to an int or float. The engine also accepts a four argument form (owner, slot, index, value) for an effect in a slot.

Original name: `_ESM_SET_VALUE`.

### set_chara_status

```c
extern void set_chara_status(enum user_chara chara, enum chara_status_attr status_bits, int clear) NATIVE(1200);
```

Changes a party character's status condition bits (1 poison, 2 slow, 0x10 power up, others unnamed). With clear = 1 the bits are removed, otherwise they are added. The engine also accepts a two argument form (status_bits, clear) for the character active in battle.

Original name: `_SET_CHARA_CONDITION`.

### add_weapon_hp

```c
extern void add_weapon_hp(enum equip_slot weapon_slot, int amount) NATIVE(1201);
```

Adds amount to the weapon durability gauge in weapon_slot of the character active in battle; negative values reduce it. For the Ridepod or a monster form it changes that gauge instead.

Original name: `_ADD_WHP`.

### add_hp_rate

```c
extern void add_hp_rate(float rate, enum hp_rate_kind mode, OPTIONAL(float frames)) NATIVE(1202);
```

Changes the active battle character's HP by rate. mode 0 scales by max HP, mode 1 by current HP; modes 2 and 3 do the same but never drop below 1. The bar animates over frames (default 0).

Original name: `_ADD_HP_RATE`.

### get_time_of_day

```c
extern float get_time_of_day(...) NATIVE(1203);
```

Returns the scene's time of day, a float from 0 to 24.

Original name: `_GET_TIME`.

### check_item_limit

```c
extern void check_item_limit(any item, OPTIONAL(int count, int *allowed)) NATIVE(1204);
```

With one argument (&over_item) it receives the number of the first item the player holds more of than its limit, or 0. With three arguments (item, count, &allowed) allowed receives count if the player can hold count more of item, otherwise 0.

Original name: `_CHECK_GET_ITEM_LIMIT`.

### check_bag_overflow

```c
extern int check_bag_overflow(void) NATIVE(1205);
```

Returns how many items sit in the overflow slots past the end of the player's current bag.

Original name: `_CHECK_ITEM_OVER`.

### get_game_mode

```c
extern int get_game_mode(void) NATIVE(1206);
```

Returns the main game mode: 1 town and georama, 2 dungeon, 3 title screen; other values are debug screens.

Original name: `_GET_NOW_LOOP_NO`.

### check_fast_clear

```c
extern int check_fast_clear(void) NATIVE(1207);
```

Compares the time since reset_floor_timer with the floor's target clear time and saves a new best. Returns 0 for no record, 1 for the first clear within the target time, 2 for a better time.

Original name: `_IS_CLEAR_DESTROY`.

### check_floor_challenge

```c
extern void check_floor_challenge(int timing, int *result, int *challenge_type) NATIVE(1208);
```

Tests the floor's practice condition. timing selects which condition kinds are tested now (the disc uses 0 and 1). result receives 0 none, 1 failed, 2 met for the first time, 3 met again; challenge_type receives the floor's condition kind (-1 for none, 6 to 9 for the parameterised kind 2).

Original name: `_IS_CLEAR_PRACTICE`.

### get_floor_sub_games

```c
extern int get_floor_sub_games(void) NATIVE(1209);
```

Returns the floor's sub games as bits: 1 spheda challenge, 2 fishing record.

Original name: `_IS_PLAY_SUB_GAME`.

### reset_floor_timer

```c
extern void reset_floor_timer(int unused) NATIVE(1210);
```

Starts timing the floor's clear time from the current play time. The argument is ignored; the disc passes -1.

Original name: `_RESET_SUBJECT_COUNTER`.

### init_screen_wave

```c
extern void init_screen_wave(float amplitude, float speed, float pitch) NATIVE(1211);
```

Resets the raster screen wave effect and sets its amplitude, speed and pitch. The disc passes speed and pitch as fractions of pi.

Original name: `_SCR_EFF_INIT_RASTER`.

### start_screen_wave

```c
extern void start_screen_wave(float amplitude, float speed, float pitch, int frames) NATIVE(1212);
```

Turns the screen wave on, moving amplitude, speed and pitch to the given values over frames frames (at once when frames is 0 or 1). A value of -1 keeps that setting.

Original name: `_SCR_EFF_START_RASTER`.

### stop_screen_wave

```c
extern void stop_screen_wave(float amplitude, float speed, float pitch, int frames) NATIVE(1213);
```

Turns the screen wave off, moving amplitude, speed and pitch to the given values over frames frames first (at once when frames is 0 or 1). A value of -1 keeps that setting.

Original name: `_SCR_EFF_STOP_RASTER`.

### set_map_part_motion

```c
extern void set_map_part_motion(any part, string piece_name, string motion_name, OPTIONAL(enum chara_motion_flag motion_flags)) NATIVE(1214);
```

Plays a motion on an animated piece of a placed map part, such as a train engine. part is the placed part's number or name, searched in every active map; piece_name names the piece inside it. motion_flags (default 0) are passed to the motion.

Original name: `_SET_MPCHARA_MOTION`.

### set_func_point_pos

```c
extern void set_func_point_pos(any part, string point_name, float x, float y, float z) NATIVE(1215);
```

Moves a function point of the active map to x, y, z. part is a placed part's number or name, or "" for the map's own function points.

Original name: `_FUNC_POINT_POS`.

### turn_camera_to

```c
extern void turn_camera_to(float angle) NATIVE(1216);
```

Turns the player camera toward the yaw angle (radians), step by step each frame until it is within 0.1 of it.

Original name: `_CTRLC_ROT_BACK`.

### compare_part_name

```c
extern int compare_part_name(int part_no, string name) NATIVE(1217);
```

Compares the part name of placed part part_no in the active map with name; returns 0 when they match, nonzero otherwise, and -1 when the part does not exist.

Original name: `_PARTS_NAME_STRCMP`.

### get_trial_version

```c
extern int get_trial_version(void) NATIVE(1218);
```

Returns 0; this build is never a trial version.

Original name: `_GET_TRIAL_VERSION`.

### show_floor_title

```c
extern void show_floor_title(int on) NATIVE(1219);
```

Shows (1) or hides (0) the current dungeon floor's title in a frame at the bottom of the screen.

Original name: `_SET_FLOOR_EPISODE`.

### get_func_point_pos

```c
extern void get_func_point_pos(any part, string point_name, float *x, float *y, float *z) NATIVE(1220);
```

Reads the position of a function point in the active map. part is a placed part's number or name, or "" for the map's own function points.

Original name: `_FUNC_POINT_GET_POS`.

### get_func_point_rot

```c
extern void get_func_point_rot(any part, string point_name, float *rot, OPTIONAL(float *rot_y, float *rot_z)) NATIVE(1221);
```

Reads the rotation of a function point in the active map, in radians. With one out argument it receives the Y rotation; with three they receive X, Y and Z. part is a placed part's number or name, or "" for the map's own points.

Original name: `_FUNC_POINT_GET_ROT`.

### play_default_motion

```c
extern void play_default_motion(int actor, OPTIONAL(enum chara_motion_flag motion_flags)) NATIVE(1222);
```

Stops the action of a scene character and plays its default motion on it and every chained part. motion_flags (default 0) are passed to the motion.

Original name: `_ACTCHR_SET_DEF_MOTION`.

### add_synth_points

```c
extern void add_synth_points(enum user_chara chara, enum equip_slot weapon_slot, int points) NATIVE(1223);
```

Adds synthesis points to the weapon in weapon_slot (0 or 1) of chara (0 or 1).

Original name: `_ADD_FUSION_POINT`.

### get_debug_flag

```c
extern int get_debug_flag(void) NATIVE(1224);
```

Returns the game's debug flag.

Original name: `_GET_DEBUG_FLAG`.

### open_minimap_door

```c
extern void open_minimap_door(float x, float y, float z) NATIVE(1225);
```

Marks the door at a world position as open on the dungeon mini map and updates the navigation map around it.

Original name: `_MINIMAP_DOOR_ENABLE`.

### check_boss_floor

```c
extern int check_boss_floor(void) NATIVE(1226);
```

Returns nonzero on a boss floor, where the mini map symbols are hidden.

Original name: `_DNG_CHECK_BOSS_MAP`.

### queue_dungeon_event

```c
extern void queue_dungeon_event(int event_no) NATIVE(1227);
```

Sets the event the dungeon runs when it can; -1 clears the request.

Original name: `_DNG_RUN_EVENT`.

### check_chara_switchable

```c
extern int check_chara_switchable(enum user_chara chara) NATIVE(1228);
```

Returns whether the player may switch to chara (0 Max, 1 Monica, 2 Ridepod, 3 Monica's monster form). It checks the unlock flags, HP, blocking status conditions and the floor's restrictions.

Original name: `_CHECK_ENABLE_CHARA_CHANGE`.

### init_sepia

```c
extern void init_sepia(enum memory_stack stack_no, OPTIONAL(int block_offset)) NATIVE(1229);
```

Prepares the sepia screen effect: a screen sized texture in the event texture block at block_offset (default 0) and an image buffer from scene memory stack stack_no, or the shared read buffer when stack_no is -1.

Original name: `_INIT_SEPIA`.

### start_sepia

```c
extern void start_sepia(void) NATIVE(1230);
```

Captures the current screen, tints it sepia and shows it until end_sepia. Needs init_sepia first.

Original name: `_START_SEPIA`.

### end_sepia

```c
extern void end_sepia(void) NATIVE(1231);
```

Turns the sepia screen effect off.

Original name: `_END_SEPIA`.

### copy_monster_to_actor

```c
extern void copy_monster_to_actor(enum memory_stack stack_no, int monster, int actor) NATIVE(1232);
```

Copies a loaded dungeon monster's model into scene character slot actor, replacing what was there. stack_no is the scene memory stack the copy is allocated from; monster is the monster's base number.

Original name: `_COPY_MONS2SCNCHR`.

### unlock_stack

```c
extern void unlock_stack(enum memory_stack stack_no) NATIVE(1233);
```

Clears the lock on scene memory stack stack_no so it can be reused.

Original name: `_UNLOCK_STACK`.

### clear_event_running

```c
extern void clear_event_running(void) NATIVE(1234);
```

Clears the scene's flag that says an event is running.

Original name: `_RESET_EVENT_TRG`.

### set_slot_chara_id

```c
extern void set_slot_chara_id(chara_slot slot, int chara_id) NATIVE(1235);
```

Sets the character id stored in scene character slot.

Original name: `_SET_CHARA_NO`.

### get_slot_chara_id

```c
extern int get_slot_chara_id(chara_slot slot) NATIVE(1236);
```

Returns the character id stored in scene character slot, or -1 when the slot does not exist.

Original name: `_GET_CHARA_NO`.

### find_villager_slot

```c
extern int find_villager_slot(npc_id chara_id) NATIVE(1237);
```

Searches the villager and talk slots of the scene for chara_id and returns its slot number, or -1.

Original name: `_SEARCH_CHARA_NO`.

### find_near_random_stone

```c

```

Looks for a random stone within 30 units of x, y, z. found receives 1 and the out position the stone's position, or found is -1 and the position is 0, 0, 0.

### init_mono_flash

```c
extern void init_mono_flash(enum memory_stack stack_no) NATIVE(1239);
```

Prepares the mono flash screen effect: two screen sized textures in the first two event texture blocks and their image buffers from scene memory stack stack_no, or the shared read buffer when stack_no is negative.

Original name: `_INIT_MONO_FLASH`.

### start_mono_flash

```c
extern void start_mono_flash(int interval) NATIVE(1240);
```

Captures the current screen and starts the mono flash effect, switching images every interval frames. Needs init_mono_flash first.

Original name: `_START_MONO_FLASH`.

### end_mono_flash

### set_chara_max_hp

### set_chara_defence

### compare_place_name

### add_chara_hp_rate

### set_bgm_time_volume

### get_chara_weapon_hp


Reads the durability of the weapon in weapon_slot (0 or 1) of chara (0 Max, 1 Monica, 2 Ridepod, 3 Monica's monster form). whp receives the current value and max, when given, the maximum.

### add_chara_weapon_abs

```c
extern void add_chara_weapon_abs(enum user_chara chara, enum equip_slot weapon_slot, int amount) NATIVE(1270);
```

### _EXT_492

```c
extern void _EXT_492(...) NATIVE(492);
```

Numbers the disc calls that this table never registers. The engine prints "not found ext" and moves on, so these calls do nothing.

### _EXT_800

```c
extern void _EXT_800(...) NATIVE(800);
```

## Helpers

A helper stands for the native calls in its body: the compiler expands it in place, and the decompiler folds every run it matches.

| Helper | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| [`init_actor`](#init_actor) | Clears the actor's sequence and makes it drive the event object handle with the same number. |
| [`init_chara_actor`](#init_chara_actor) | Binds a handle to a scene character and sets up the actor of the same number to drive it. |
| [`init_sprite_actor`](#init_sprite_actor) | Binds a handle to an event sprite and sets up the actor of the same number to drive it. |
| [`init_map_piece_actor`](#init_map_piece_actor) | Binds a handle to a named piece of a map part and sets up the actor of the same number to drive it. |
| [`init_map_part_actor`](#init_map_part_actor) | Binds a handle to a map part and sets up the actor of the same number to drive it. |
| [`bind_actor_to_chara`](#bind_actor_to_chara) | Binds a handle to a scene character and sets up an actor with a different number to drive that handle. |
| [`activate_chara_actor`](#activate_chara_actor) | Activates a scene character, binds a handle to it and sets up the actor of the same number to drive it. |
| [`load_chara_actor`](#load_chara_actor) | Loads a character pack on memory stack 5, activates it and sets up the handle and actor of the same number to drive it. |
| [`load_scene_chara`](#load_scene_chara) | Loads a character pack into a scene character slot on memory stack 5. |
| [`load_active_chara`](#load_active_chara) | Loads a character pack into a scene character slot on memory stack 5 and activates it. |
| [`load_inactive_chara`](#load_inactive_chara) | Loads a character pack into a scene character slot on memory stack 5 and leaves it inactive. |
| [`add_chara_motions`](#add_chara_motions) | Loads an extra motion pack into a scene character on memory stack 5. |
| [`load_party_equipment`](#load_party_equipment) | Loads a party member's current skins onto an event character on memory stack 5, and its weapon as character weapon. |
| [`load_party_member`](#load_party_member) | Loads a party member's base pack into a scene character on memory stack 5 with its current skins, and its weapon as character weapon. |
| [`activate_chara`](#activate_chara) | Makes a scene character active. |
| [`deactivate_chara`](#deactivate_chara) | Makes a scene character inactive, so it is neither stepped nor drawn. |
| [`place_actor`](#place_actor) | Queues putting the actor at a world position facing the yaw angle in radians. |
| [`warp_actor`](#warp_actor) | Queues putting the actor at a world position facing the yaw angle in radians and settles its hair and cloth there. |
| [`set_actor_yaw`](#set_actor_yaw) | Queues turning the actor to a yaw angle in radians, level on the other axes. |
| [`turn_actor_to_yaw`](#turn_actor_to_yaw) | Queues turning the actor to a yaw angle in radians over a number of frames, level on the other axes. |
| [`place_chara`](#place_chara) | Places a scene character at a world position facing the yaw angle in radians. |
| [`set_chara_yaw`](#set_chara_yaw) | Turns a scene character to a yaw angle in radians, level on the other axes. |
| [`place_handle`](#place_handle) | Moves the object bound to a handle to a position facing the yaw angle in radians. |
| [`set_handle_yaw`](#set_handle_yaw) | Turns the object bound to a handle to a yaw angle in radians, level on the other axes. |
| [`play_handle_motion`](#play_handle_motion) | Starts a motion from its first frame on the character bound to a handle. |
| [`set_camera_view`](#set_camera_view) | Queues putting the camera eye and look-at point at two world positions at once. |
| [`cut_camera`](#cut_camera) | Drops all queued camera commands, then queues putting the camera eye and look-at point at two world positions at once. |
| [`begin_camera_path`](#begin_camera_path) | Queues clearing the camera path and setting how many frames the camera takes to run the new one. |
| [`play_actor_motions`](#play_actor_motions) | Queues a motion on the actor with the given flags and the motion that follows it with its own flags. |
| [`play_actor_motion_then_loop`](#play_actor_motion_then_loop) | Queues a motion on the actor from its first frame, stopping on its last frame, then loops the next motion from its first frame. |
| [`loop_actor_motion`](#loop_actor_motion) | Queues a looping motion on the actor from its first frame. |
| [`play_actor_motion_once`](#play_actor_motion_once) | Queues a motion on the actor from its first frame that stops on its last frame. |
| [`snap_actor_motion`](#snap_actor_motion) | Queues a looping motion on the actor from its first frame and shows its first pose at once. |
| [`switch_lip_sync_anim`](#switch_lip_sync_anim) | Clears the lip sync setup, then makes the actor's talk animation the moving mouth. |
| [`set_actor_face`](#set_actor_face) | Stops the actor's texture animations, then plays a face and an eye animation. |
| [`stop_actor_texture_anims`](#stop_actor_texture_anims) | Queues stopping all of the actor's texture animations. |
| [`switch_actor_texture_anim`](#switch_actor_texture_anim) | Stops the actor's texture animations, then plays one named animation. |
| [`play_actor_texture_anim`](#play_actor_texture_anim) | Queues playing a named texture animation on the actor, such as a face or eye-blink loop. |
| [`set_handle_face`](#set_handle_face) | Stops the texture animations of the character bound to a handle, then plays a face and an eye animation. |
| [`stop_handle_texture_anims`](#stop_handle_texture_anims) | Stops all texture animations of the character bound to a handle. |
| [`switch_handle_texture_anim`](#switch_handle_texture_anim) | Stops the texture animations of the character bound to a handle, then plays one named animation. |
| [`play_handle_texture_anim`](#play_handle_texture_anim) | Plays a named texture animation on the character bound to a handle. |
| [`set_message_style`](#set_message_style) | Sets a message window's frame style and how many characters it reveals per frame. |
| [`show_map_part`](#show_map_part) | Binds a handle to a map part and shows it. |
| [`hide_map_part`](#hide_map_part) | Binds a handle to a map part and hides it. |
| [`show_map_piece`](#show_map_piece) | Binds a handle to a named piece of a map part and shows it. |
| [`hide_map_piece`](#hide_map_piece) | Binds a handle to a named piece of a map part and hides it. |
| [`init_screen_sprite`](#init_screen_sprite) | Resets an event sprite to a screen sprite showing a texture from a texture block. |
| [`set_sprite_alpha`](#set_sprite_alpha) | Sets an event sprite to its full colour with the given alpha, 128 being opaque. |
| [`crop_sprite`](#crop_sprite) | Shows a width by height region of the sprite's texture starting at u, v, drawn at that same size. |
| [`begin_actor_path`](#begin_actor_path) | Queues clearing the actor's walking path and setting how many frames it takes to walk the new one. |
| [`play_actor_se_after`](#play_actor_se_after) | Queues a sound effect on the actor's sound effect track after waiting a number of frames. |
| [`reset_memory_stack`](#reset_memory_stack) | Gives a memory stack the free space below it and empties it. |
| [`start_bgm`](#start_bgm) | Loads a music file and plays its first track. |

### init_actor

Clears the actor's sequence and makes it drive the event object handle with the same number.

```c
static inline void init_actor(int actor)
{
    clear_actor_seq(actor);
    bind_actor_object(actor, actor);
}
```

### init_chara_actor

Binds a handle to a scene character and sets up the actor of the same number to drive it.

```c
static inline void init_chara_actor(int actor, chara_slot chara)
{
    bind_handle_chara(actor, chara);
    clear_actor_seq(actor);
    bind_actor_object(actor, actor);
}
```

### init_sprite_actor

Binds a handle to an event sprite and sets up the actor of the same number to drive it.

```c
static inline void init_sprite_actor(int actor, int sprite)
{
    bind_handle_sprite(actor, sprite);
    clear_actor_seq(actor);
    bind_actor_object(actor, actor);
}
```

### init_map_piece_actor

Binds a handle to a named piece of a map part and sets up the actor of the same number to drive it.

```c
static inline void init_map_piece_actor(int actor, any part, string piece)
{
    bind_handle_map_part(actor, part, piece);
    clear_actor_seq(actor);
    bind_actor_object(actor, actor);
}
```

### init_map_part_actor

Binds a handle to a map part and sets up the actor of the same number to drive it.

```c
static inline void init_map_part_actor(int actor, any part)
{
    bind_handle_map_part(actor, part);
    clear_actor_seq(actor);
    bind_actor_object(actor, actor);
}
```

### bind_actor_to_chara

Binds a handle to a scene character and sets up an actor with a different number to drive that handle.

```c
static inline void bind_actor_to_chara(int actor, int handle, chara_slot chara)
{
    bind_handle_chara(handle, chara);
    clear_actor_seq(actor, chara);
    bind_actor_object(actor, handle);
}
```

### activate_chara_actor

Activates a scene character, binds a handle to it and sets up the actor of the same number to drive it.

```c
static inline void activate_chara_actor(int actor, chara_slot chara)
{
    set_chara_active(chara);
    bind_handle_chara(actor, chara);
    clear_actor_seq(actor);
    bind_actor_object(actor, actor);
}
```

### load_chara_actor

Loads a character pack on memory stack 5, activates it and sets up the handle and actor of the same number to drive it.

```c
static inline void load_chara_actor(int actor, string pack, chara_slot chara)
{
    load_chara(5, pack, "info.cfg", chara);
    set_chara_active(chara);
    bind_handle_chara(actor, chara);
    clear_actor_seq(actor);
    bind_actor_object(actor, actor);
}
```

### load_scene_chara

Loads a character pack into a scene character slot on memory stack 5.

```c
static inline void load_scene_chara(chara_slot chara, string pack)
{
    load_chara(5, pack, "info.cfg", chara);
}
```

### load_active_chara

Loads a character pack into a scene character slot on memory stack 5 and activates it.

```c
static inline void load_active_chara(chara_slot chara, string pack)
{
    load_chara(5, pack, "info.cfg", chara);
    set_chara_active(chara);
}
```

### load_inactive_chara

Loads a character pack into a scene character slot on memory stack 5 and leaves it inactive.

```c
static inline void load_inactive_chara(chara_slot chara, string pack)
{
    load_chara(5, pack, "info.cfg", chara);
    set_chara_active(0, chara);
}
```

### add_chara_motions

Loads an extra motion pack into a scene character on memory stack 5.

```c
static inline void add_chara_motions(chara_slot chara, string pack)
{
    load_chara_motion(5, pack, "info.cfg", chara);
}
```

### load_party_equipment

Loads a party member's current skins onto an event character on memory stack 5, and its weapon as character weapon.

```c
static inline void load_party_equipment(int member, chara_slot chara, int weapon)
{
    load_equip_model(5, member, 4, "info.cfg", chara);
    load_equip_model(5, member, 3, "info.cfg", chara);
    load_equip_model(5, member, 2, "info.cfg", chara, weapon);
}
```

### load_party_member

Loads a party member's base pack into a scene character on memory stack 5 with its current skins, and its weapon as character weapon.

```c
static inline void load_party_member(int member, string pack, chara_slot chara, int weapon)
{
    load_chara(5, pack, "info.cfg", chara);
    load_equip_model(5, member, 4, "info.cfg", chara);
    load_equip_model(5, member, 3, "info.cfg", chara);
    load_equip_model(5, member, 2, "info.cfg", chara, weapon);
}
```

### activate_chara

Makes a scene character active.

```c
static inline void activate_chara(chara_slot chara)
{
    set_chara_active(1, chara);
}
```

### deactivate_chara

Makes a scene character inactive, so it is neither stepped nor drawn.

```c
static inline void deactivate_chara(chara_slot chara)
{
    set_chara_active(0, chara);
}
```

### place_actor

Queues putting the actor at a world position facing the yaw angle in radians.

```c
static inline void place_actor(int actor, float x, float y, float z, float yaw)
{
    set_actor_pos(actor, x, y, z);
    set_actor_rot(actor, 0.0, yaw, 0.0);
}
```

### warp_actor

Queues putting the actor at a world position facing the yaw angle in radians and settles its hair and cloth there.

```c
static inline void warp_actor(int actor, float x, float y, float z, float yaw)
{
    set_actor_pos(actor, x, y, z);
    set_actor_rot(actor, 0.0, yaw, 0.0);
    reset_actor_dynamics(actor);
}
```

### set_actor_yaw

Queues turning the actor to a yaw angle in radians, level on the other axes.

```c
static inline void set_actor_yaw(int actor, float yaw)
{
    set_actor_rot(actor, 0.0, yaw, 0.0);
}
```

### turn_actor_to_yaw

Queues turning the actor to a yaw angle in radians over a number of frames, level on the other axes.

```c
static inline void turn_actor_to_yaw(int actor, float yaw, int frames)
{
    turn_actor_to(actor, 0.0, yaw, 0.0, frames);
}
```

### place_chara

Places a scene character at a world position facing the yaw angle in radians.

```c
static inline void place_chara(chara_slot chara, float x, float y, float z, float yaw)
{
    set_chara_pos(chara, x, y, z);
    set_chara_rot(chara, 0.0, yaw, 0.0);
}
```

### set_chara_yaw

Turns a scene character to a yaw angle in radians, level on the other axes.

```c
static inline void set_chara_yaw(chara_slot chara, float yaw)
{
    set_chara_rot(chara, 0.0, yaw, 0.0);
}
```

### place_handle

Moves the object bound to a handle to a position facing the yaw angle in radians.

```c
static inline void place_handle(int handle, float x, float y, float z, float yaw)
{
    set_handle_pos(handle, x, y, z);
    set_handle_rot(handle, 0.0, yaw, 0.0);
}
```

### set_handle_yaw

Turns the object bound to a handle to a yaw angle in radians, level on the other axes.

```c
static inline void set_handle_yaw(int handle, float yaw)
{
    set_handle_rot(handle, 0.0, yaw, 0.0);
}
```

### play_handle_motion

Starts a motion from its first frame on the character bound to a handle.

```c
static inline void play_handle_motion(int handle, string motion)
{
    set_handle_motion(handle, motion, CHARA_MOTION_RESTART);
}
```

### set_camera_view

Queues putting the camera eye and look-at point at two world positions at once.

```c
static inline void set_camera_view(float x, float y, float z, float ref_x, float ref_y, float ref_z)
{
    set_camera_seq_pos(x, y, z);
    set_camera_seq_ref(ref_x, ref_y, ref_z);
}
```

### cut_camera

Drops all queued camera commands, then queues putting the camera eye and look-at point at two world positions at once.

```c
static inline void cut_camera(float x, float y, float z, float ref_x, float ref_y, float ref_z)
{
    clear_camera_seq();
    set_camera_seq_pos(x, y, z);
    set_camera_seq_ref(ref_x, ref_y, ref_z);
}
```

### begin_camera_path

Queues clearing the camera path and setting how many frames the camera takes to run the new one.

```c
static inline void begin_camera_path(int frames)
{
    clear_camera_path();
    set_camera_path_frames(frames);
}
```

### play_actor_motions

Queues a motion on the actor with the given flags and the motion that follows it with its own flags.

```c
static inline void play_actor_motions(int actor, string motion, enum chara_motion_flag flags, string next, enum chara_motion_flag next_flags)
{
    set_actor_motion(actor, motion, flags);
    queue_actor_motion(actor, next, next_flags);
}
```

### play_actor_motion_then_loop

Queues a motion on the actor from its first frame, stopping on its last frame, then loops the next motion from its first frame.

```c
static inline void play_actor_motion_then_loop(int actor, string motion, string next)
{
    set_actor_motion(actor, motion, CHARA_MOTION_RESTART | CHARA_MOTION_HOLD);
    queue_actor_motion(actor, next, CHARA_MOTION_RESTART);
}
```

### loop_actor_motion

Queues a looping motion on the actor from its first frame.

```c
static inline void loop_actor_motion(int actor, string motion)
{
    set_actor_motion(actor, motion, CHARA_MOTION_RESTART);
}
```

### play_actor_motion_once

Queues a motion on the actor from its first frame that stops on its last frame.

```c
static inline void play_actor_motion_once(int actor, string motion)
{
    set_actor_motion(actor, motion, CHARA_MOTION_RESTART | CHARA_MOTION_HOLD);
}
```

### snap_actor_motion

Queues a looping motion on the actor from its first frame and shows its first pose at once.

```c
static inline void snap_actor_motion(int actor, string motion)
{
    set_actor_motion(actor, motion, CHARA_MOTION_RESTART, -1.0, 1);
}
```

### switch_lip_sync_anim

Clears the lip sync setup, then makes the actor's talk animation the moving mouth.

```c
static inline void switch_lip_sync_anim(int actor, string talk_anim)
{
    reset_lip_sync_anim();
    set_lip_sync_anim(actor, talk_anim);
}
```

### set_actor_face

Stops the actor's texture animations, then plays a face and an eye animation.

```c
static inline void set_actor_face(int actor, string face, string eyes)
{
    set_actor_texture_anim(actor, 0);
    set_actor_texture_anim(actor, 1, face);
    set_actor_texture_anim(actor, 1, eyes);
}
```

### stop_actor_texture_anims

Queues stopping all of the actor's texture animations.

```c
static inline void stop_actor_texture_anims(int actor)
{
    set_actor_texture_anim(actor, 0);
}
```

### switch_actor_texture_anim

Stops the actor's texture animations, then plays one named animation.

```c
static inline void switch_actor_texture_anim(int actor, string anim)
{
    set_actor_texture_anim(actor, 0);
    set_actor_texture_anim(actor, 1, anim);
}
```

### play_actor_texture_anim

Queues playing a named texture animation on the actor, such as a face or eye-blink loop.

```c
static inline void play_actor_texture_anim(int actor, string anim)
{
    set_actor_texture_anim(actor, 1, anim);
}
```

### set_handle_face

Stops the texture animations of the character bound to a handle, then plays a face and an eye animation.

```c
static inline void set_handle_face(int handle, string face, string eyes)
{
    set_handle_texture_anim(handle, 0);
    set_handle_texture_anim(handle, 1, face);
    set_handle_texture_anim(handle, 1, eyes);
}
```

### stop_handle_texture_anims

Stops all texture animations of the character bound to a handle.

```c
static inline void stop_handle_texture_anims(int handle)
{
    set_handle_texture_anim(handle, 0);
}
```

### switch_handle_texture_anim

Stops the texture animations of the character bound to a handle, then plays one named animation.

```c
static inline void switch_handle_texture_anim(int handle, string anim)
{
    set_handle_texture_anim(handle, 0);
    set_handle_texture_anim(handle, 1, anim);
}
```

### play_handle_texture_anim

Plays a named texture animation on the character bound to a handle.

```c
static inline void play_handle_texture_anim(int handle, string anim)
{
    set_handle_texture_anim(handle, 1, anim);
}
```

### set_message_style

Sets a message window's frame style and how many characters it reveals per frame.

```c
static inline void set_message_style(int window, enum mes_window_mode mode, float speed)
{
    set_message_window_mode(window, mode);
    set_message_speed(window, speed);
}
```

### show_map_part

Binds a handle to a map part and shows it.

```c
static inline void show_map_part(int handle, any part)
{
    bind_handle_map_part(handle, part);
    set_handle_visible(handle, 1);
}
```

### hide_map_part

Binds a handle to a map part and hides it.

```c
static inline void hide_map_part(int handle, any part)
{
    bind_handle_map_part(handle, part);
    set_handle_visible(handle, 0);
}
```

### show_map_piece

Binds a handle to a named piece of a map part and shows it.

```c
static inline void show_map_piece(int handle, any part, string piece)
{
    bind_handle_map_part(handle, part, piece);
    set_handle_visible(handle, 1);
}
```

### hide_map_piece

Binds a handle to a named piece of a map part and hides it.

```c
static inline void hide_map_piece(int handle, any part, string piece)
{
    bind_handle_map_part(handle, part, piece);
    set_handle_visible(handle, 0);
}
```

### init_screen_sprite

Resets an event sprite to a screen sprite showing a texture from a texture block.

```c
static inline void init_screen_sprite(int sprite, string texture, int tex_block)
{
    init_sprite(sprite);
    set_sprite_type(sprite, EVENT_SPRITE2_TYPE_SCREEN);
    set_sprite_texture(sprite, texture, tex_block);
}
```

### set_sprite_alpha

Sets an event sprite to its full colour with the given alpha, 128 being opaque.

```c
static inline void set_sprite_alpha(int sprite, float alpha)
{
    set_sprite_color(sprite, 128, 128, 128, alpha);
}
```

### crop_sprite

Shows a width by height region of the sprite's texture starting at u, v, drawn at that same size.

```c
static inline void crop_sprite(int sprite, int u, int v, int width, int height)
{
    set_sprite_uv(sprite, u, v, width, height);
    set_sprite_size(sprite, width, height);
}
```

### begin_actor_path

Queues clearing the actor's walking path and setting how many frames it takes to walk the new one.

```c
static inline void begin_actor_path(int actor, int frames)
{
    clear_actor_path(actor);
    set_actor_path_frames(actor, frames);
}
```

### play_actor_se_after

Queues a sound effect on the actor's sound effect track after waiting a number of frames.

```c
static inline void play_actor_se_after(int actor, int frames, int sound, int se_no)
{
    delay_actor_se(actor, frames);
    play_actor_se(actor, sound, se_no);
}
```

### reset_memory_stack

Gives a memory stack the free space below it and empties it.

```c
static inline void reset_memory_stack(enum memory_stack stack_no)
{
    assign_memory_stack(stack_no);
    clear_memory_stack(stack_no);
}
```

### start_bgm

Loads a music file and plays its first track.

```c
static inline void start_bgm(int bgm_file)
{
    load_bgm(bgm_file);
    play_bgm(0);
}
```

## Programs

The program numbers the engine starts (`enum program`): a script lists the ones it answers with `PROGRAM(name, function);`.

| Program | Number | When it runs |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `setup` | 99 | Sets the map up from story flags without starting a scene: most retail bodies only set objects and flags without calling \_INITIALIZE, some program 100 functions call the same function first (s01), and the engine starts it directly only from the debug future-map select (mainloop3.cpp). |
| `map_start` | 100 | Start event of a town, field or interior map: the edit loop runs it on entry when no other event is named (editloop.cpp EditInit and EditMapJump), \_MAP_JUMP, \_GOTO_INTERIOR, \_GOTO_OUTSIDE and \_FUNCTION_MAP_JUMP default to it, and retail scripts print "start_event" there; in dungeon scripts it is only the first value of the debug run-event command (dng_debug.cpp command_int). |
| `debug_test` | 150 | Developer test hook: the edit loop reloads the map script and runs 150 when Square is pressed on the second controller (editloop.cpp kKeyOpenEvent, GamePad Down2), and scripts reuse it as a \_LOAD_SCRIPT target. |
| `board_train` | 250 | Train door function point on station maps (s02, s04, s09-s12, s31, s34, i02h01): retail bodies ask whether to depart and then jump to the destination map. |
| `gate` | 300 | Gate event: in towns the engine sends point number 300 when the player stands in the coloured time gate marker (CScene::GetMapEvent, SCENE_GAMEOBJ_SLOT_TG) and scripts warp through it, and on dungeon floors 300 is the first "door01" function point that needs an item to open. |
| `key_door` | 348 | Locked door of a dungeon floor part: every "key" function point in the dungeon maps carries point number 348, and retail bodies ask to use item 340 to open it. |
| `fish_race_end` | 350 | Runs when a fish race in the gyorace subgame ends normally (gyorace.cpp RunEvent(350)); defined only by i03h05. |
| `fish_race_end_bonus` | 352 | Runs when a fish race ends in the title-screen bonus mode (gyorace.cpp RunEvent(352) when OmakeFlag is set); defined only by i03h05. |
| `save_point` | 400 | Save point: in towns the engine sends point number 400 when the player stands at the save point game object (CScene::GetMapEvent, SCENE_GAMEOBJ_SLOT_SAVEPOINT), and most retail bodies open the menu (open_menu_faded). |
| `talk_or_dungeon_start` | 1000 | In towns the edit loop runs it when the player presses the confirm button next to a character (editloop.cpp GetTalkEvent then RunEvent(1000)); in dungeon scripts it is the default start event of the dungeon loop, which retail scripts use to pick the floor from the menu (dng_main.cpp sets event_no 1000 when none is given). |
| `floor_start` | 1010 | Starts a dungeon floor whose number is already chosen: \_GOTO_DNG from the town gate menu, the new game and save-load paths in title.cpp and the edit-loop menu jump pass 1010, and retail bodies set the floor up (and program 1000 ends by calling it). |
| `treasure_chest` | 1100 | Runs when the player presses confirm at a treasure chest on a dungeon floor (dng_main.cpp TreasureBoxMan-\>CheckEvent). |
| `random_circle` | 1120 | Runs when the player steps onto one of the floor's random circles (dng_main.cpp RandomCircle.CheckEvent). |
| `geostone` | 1130 | Runs when the player presses confirm at the floor's geostone (dng_main.cpp GeoStone.CheckEvent). |
| `monster_talk` | 1150 | Runs when the player presses confirm or square next to a monster that can talk (dng_main.cpp CheckMonsterTolk, which also fills EdEventInfo.monster_talk). |
| `player_dead` | 1200 | Runs when the controlled character's HP reaches zero (actionchara.cpp queues battle_area event 1200 with ACTION_PROG_DEAD), and retail bodies run the game-over sequence. |
| `monsters_cleared` | 1500 | Runs once when no monsters or mimics are left on the floor or boss map (dng_main.cpp sets battle_clear). |
| `random_stone_help` | 2500 | One-time help shown the first time Max or Monica presses confirm at a random stone (dng_main.cpp SearchRandomStone, save bit flag 0x32). |
| `monster_help` | 2510 | One-time help requested by the e202a monster script through \_RUN_EVENT_SCRIPT(2510) when its attack type is 0 (guarded by bit flag 51); retail bodies show dungeon message 2610. |
| `level_up_help` | 2520 | One-time help shown at the first level up in a dungeon (dng_main.cpp LevelupInfo.phase, save bit flag 0x34). |
| `robot_bike_help` | 2530 | One-time help shown the first time the robot character moves in its bike form (dng_main.cpp USER_CHARA_ROBO with move_type 3, SAVE_FLAG_ROBO_BIKE_EVENT_SEEN). |
| `magic_sword_help` | 2540 | One-time help shown the first time Monica's magic sword gauge holds a charge (dng_main.cpp GetMagicSwordCounterNow, save bit flag 0x3B). |
| `spheda_shot` | 3000 | Spheda: the player stands at the ball and presses the action button (sphida.cpp SPHIDA_EVENT_SHOT). |
| `spheda_menu` | 3001 | Spheda: the player presses the menu button near the ball (sphida.cpp SPHIDA_EVENT_NEAR_BALL). |
| `spheda_menu_last` | 3002 | Spheda: as spheda_menu with fewer than two shots of par left (sphida.cpp SPHIDA_EVENT_NEAR_BALL_LAST). |
| `spheda_menu_away` | 3003 | Spheda bonus courses: the player presses the menu button away from the ball (sphida.cpp SPHIDA_EVENT_OMAKE_AWAY). |
| `spheda_debug_start` | 4000 | Spheda test start: the debug subgame menu enters dungeon map 2 floor 1 with event 4000 (mainloop.cpp), and retail bodies load the floor and call setup_spheda. |
| `spheda_bonus_menu` | 6000 | Start of the title-screen bonus dungeon mode (title.cpp InitOmakeEnv, OMAKE_TYPE_DUNGEON), where retail bodies open the Spheda course menu; the \*\_sfida scripts also \_LOAD_SCRIPT into it. |
| `spheda_bonus_course` | 6001 | Loads a Spheda bonus hole after the course menu: \*\_sfida scripts start it with \_LOAD_SCRIPT and retail bodies place the ball and call setup_spheda. |
| `story_1` | 10010 | Story event 1 「OPENING PART１」 (opening part 1) on map m07, from the debug event list event/view_pal.txt, whose program numbers are 10000 + 10 x event + part. |
| `story_2` | 10020 | Story event 2 「OPENING PART１.5」 (opening part 1.5) on map m06, from the debug event list event/view_pal.txt, whose program numbers are 10000 + 10 x event + part. |
| `story_3` | 10030 | Story event 3 「OPENING PART2」 (opening part 2) on dungeon 1 floor 21, from the debug event list event/view_pal.txt, whose program numbers are 10000 + 10 x event + part. |
| `story_4` | 10040 | Story event 4 「OPENING PART3」 (opening part 3) on dungeon 1 floor 21, from the debug event list event/view_pal.txt, whose program numbers are 10000 + 10 x event + part. |
| `story_5` | 10050 | Story event 5 「OPENING PART4」 (opening part 4) on dungeon 1 floor 21, from the debug event list event/view_pal.txt, whose program numbers are 10000 + 10 x event + part. |
| `story_6` | 10060 | Story event 6 「OPENING PART5」 (opening part 5) on map m06, from the debug event list event/view_pal.txt, whose program numbers are 10000 + 10 x event + part. |
| `story_7` | 10070 | Story event 7 「OPENING PART6」 (opening part 6) on map d01e01, from the debug event list event/view_pal.txt, whose program numbers are 10000 + 10 x event + part. |
| `story_50` | 10500 | Story event 50 「プロローグ・モニカの戦い」 (prologue, Monica's battle) on dungeon 1 floor 20, from the debug event list event/view_pal.txt, whose program numbers are 10000 + 10 x event + part. |
| `story_100` | 11000 | Story event 100 「ダンジョンでの最初の通信」 (first call in the dungeon) on dungeon 1 floor 1, from the debug event list event/view_pal.txt, whose program numbers are 10000 + 10 x event + part. |

Bits of the save data's 8-bit control value CSaveData::bit_ctrl: 1 blocks the world and dungeon map moves (menumain.cpp message 0x2A), 2 blocks Georama edit mode (editloop.cpp), 8 hides the Atlamillia on loaded characters (charasetup.cpp, \_LOAD_CHARA), 0x10 opens the dungeon floor map in place of the world map move (SetCommonMenuModeID).

| Name | Value |
| --- | --- |
| `BIT_CTRL_NO_MAP_MOVE` | `1` |
| `BIT_CTRL_NO_EDIT` | `2` |
| `BIT_CTRL_HIDE_ATLAMILLIA` | `8` |
| `BIT_CTRL_DUNGEON_MAP_MOVE` | `16` |

### enum memory_stack

Scene memory stack a load allocates from (CScene::stack\[], bound in editloop.cpp for town and dng_main.cpp for dungeons): 0 player characters, 1 main map data, 2 main map characters, 3 sub data, 4 sub map characters, 5 to 8 the four event buffers. Clearing a stack also clears every stack above it.

| Name | Value |
| --- | --- |
| `MEMORY_STACK_PLAYER` | `0` |
| `MEMORY_STACK_MAIN_DATA` | `1` |
| `MEMORY_STACK_MAIN_CHARA` | `2` |
| `MEMORY_STACK_SUB_DATA` | `3` |
| `MEMORY_STACK_SUB_CHARA` | `4` |
| `MEMORY_STACK_EVENT` | `5` |
| `MEMORY_STACK_EVENT_2` | `6` |
| `MEMORY_STACK_EVENT_3` | `7` |
| `MEMORY_STACK_EVENT_4` | `8` |

Vertical position on that 512 x 416 screen.

Colour and alpha level of an event sprite, where 128 is 1.0: full alpha, and a colour that leaves the texture as it is (eventsprite.cpp CEventSprite2 defaults).

| --- | --- |
| `COLOR_FULL` | `128` |

## Value kinds

What a parameter's value means. A kind reads as its type; the decompiler uses it to name or comment the values (see [the SDK](../sdk.md#value-kinds)).

| Kind | Type | What it is |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `message` | `any` | Value kinds: what a parameter's value means. They read as their base type; the decompiler uses them to comment values (a message id gets the line it shows, read from the disc). |
| `message_file` | `string` |  |
| `directory` | `string` |  |
| `item_id` | `int` |  |
| `map_id` | `any` |  |
| `flag` | `int` |  |
| `bgm_file` | `int` |  |
| `sound_set` | `int` |  |
| `system_se` | `int` |  |
| `chara_slot` | `int` |  |
| `npc_id` | `int` |  |
| `monster_kind` | `int` |  |
| `chara_pack` | `string` |  |
